=== add docx-helper/TODO.md
new 052dd3de049938ca83352862
+# Word (.docx) support — how it works and what is left
+
+Rebuilt 2026-11-17. The developer: "I never got the write to work very well. Always seemed like it was corrupting
+something unless it was a very small edit ... the part we had most issue w/ before is edits that spanned across levels.
+So if a sentence dropped down into bold, then back up".
+
+## What was wrong with the POI version (measured, Word as the judge)
+Word opened every file it wrote, but the content was damaged:
+- the reader saw only the first text piece of each run: tabs, line breaks, content-control values and footnotes were
+  invisible, and edits near them deleted them;
+- a run split put the split-off text on the wrong side of a content control ("Current [4.2.1]version: ");
+- a replace covering a link's whole text left an empty link, which Word shows as its URL;
+- a replace across a format change put all its text in the first run's format (bold words went plain);
+- two tracked edits in one paragraph crashed (POI's run list went stale after the first).
+
+## How it works now (JDK only: zip + XML, no POI)
+- `Pkg`: the .docx as zip entries. Only edited parts are written again; every other entry is copied byte for byte.
+- `Para`: a paragraph as a list of pieces - each character with its run formatting and its containers (link, content
+  control, tracked change), tabs, breaks, objects (⟦image 1⟧, ⟦footnote 2⟧), whole fields (shown as their result,
+  never cut), zero-width markers (bookmarks, comment anchors). An edit changes the list; the paragraph's content is
+  then written again from it.
+- Replace = a word-level diff of the old text against the new: kept words keep their runs; new words take the format
+  of what they replace (or, a pure insertion, of the text before it in the same container - typing at a link's edge
+  goes outside it, as in Word). Objects must be kept (refused otherwise); ⟦…⟧ cannot be added by text.
+- Tracked: the same diff written as w:del / w:ins; formatting as rPrChange; paragraph formatting as pPrChange.
+- Checks before one byte is written (`Doc.edit`): each edited paragraph is read back and must hold exactly the planned
+  pieces; every other paragraph is unchanged; the structure between paragraphs is unchanged; the package written is
+  read back. Any failure writes nothing and says it is a bug.
+- `DocxHelper serve`: one JVM, a request per line (the cb.jgit.Serve pattern). `src/agent/docx.mjs` compiles the
+  embedded sources with the JDK it runs on, starts the service, and gives the snippet functions docxRead, docxSearch,
+  docxEdit (src/agent/tools.mjs).
+
+## Tests
+- `node test/test-docx.mjs` - read, edits across formatting, tracked, refusals, structure, package, through the worker.
+- `node test/_docx-word-stress.mjs` (a box with Word) - several hundred edits on a Word-made document, each opened by
+  Word with repair off, its text compared and its formatting (as Word kept it) checked outside the edit.
+
+## Tables and new documents (added 2026-11-17)
+- `insertRow` / `deleteRow` name a row by any paragraph in it. A new row copies a body row's look (a header row's only
+  with `like`); inside a cell merged down it joins the merge (text there is refused). Deleting the top of a merge moves
+  its text to the next row. Rows with a footnote, a comment, or one end of a bookmark are refused. Tracked: w:trPr
+  w:ins / w:del.
+- `insertTable` (rows of cell texts; Table Grid when the document has it, else plain borders; a header row that
+  repeats). A paragraph is added after a table that would end the document or a cell, or touch another table.
+- `insert` / `list` take `list: "bullet" | "number"` (a new numbered list starts at 1); `insert` / `insertTable` take
+  `at: "end" | "start"`.
+- The structure check undoes this edit's rows and tables on a copy (an undo log) and needs the original back.
+- `create`: Word's blank document (Blank.java) or a template (.dotx / .docx; empty:true drops its text), then content
+  blocks through the same checked edit.
+
+## Left to do
+1. Columns (add/remove), merging and splitting cells, deleting a whole table.
+2. Edits ON text that already has tracked changes (refused now: accept or reject in Word first).
+3. Moving paragraphs (insert + delete works today, tracked or not).
+4. Text in text boxes and shapes (read and edit) - shown only as ⟦image⟧ today.
+5. Images: adding one (a file into word/media, a drawing run).
+

+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
+<project xmlns="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0"
+         xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
+         xsi:schemaLocation="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0 http://maven.apache.org/xsd/maven-4.0.0.xsd">
+  <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>
+
+  <groupId>com.codeboss</groupId>
+  <artifactId>codeboss-docx-helper</artifactId>
+  <version>2.0.0</version>
+  <packaging>jar</packaging>
+
+  <!--
+    code_boss's Word (.docx) helper: read, search and edit, with the JDK alone (java.util.zip + the JDK's XML parser).
+    No dependencies - since 2026-11-17 it no longer uses Apache POI, so nothing has to be fetched or carried to a box.
+
+    code_boss does not need this build: the bundle embeds these sources and src/agent/docx.mjs compiles them with the
+    JDK it runs on (once per version, into ~/.code_boss/docx-helper/classes-<hash>). This pom is for working on the
+    helper on its own:  mvn -o package  ->  target/codeboss-docx-helper.jar
+      java -jar target/codeboss-docx-helper.jar serve        (one request per line on stdin)
+    Tests: node test/test-docx.mjs; the Word-judged run, on a box with Word: node test/_docx-word-stress.mjs
+  -->
+
+  <properties>
+    <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>
+    <!-- Java 8: the container's oldest JDK runs it -->
+    <maven.compiler.release>8</maven.compiler.release>
+  </properties>
+
+  <build>
+    <finalName>codeboss-docx-helper</finalName>
+    <plugins>
+      <plugin>
+        <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
+        <artifactId>maven-jar-plugin</artifactId>
+        <configuration>
+          <archive>
+            <manifest>
+              <mainClass>com.codeboss.docx.DocxHelper</mainClass>
+            </manifest>
+          </archive>
+        </configuration>
+      </plugin>
+    </plugins>
+  </build>
+</project>
+
=== add docx-helper/src/main/java/com/codeboss/docx/Blank.java
new fb7847fe9c229d73a4d80fae
+package com.codeboss.docx;
+
+import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
+import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
+import java.util.zip.ZipEntry;
+import java.util.zip.ZipOutputStream;
+
+/**
+ * A new, empty Word document when no template is given: Word's own default look (Calibri 11, US Letter, 1" margins),
+ * the built-in styles a spec or a runbook uses (Title, Heading 1-4, List Paragraph, Table Grid, Hyperlink), and a
+ * bullet and a numbered list ready in numbering.xml. Built-in styles carry Word's built-in names ("heading 1"), so
+ * Word treats them as its own (a table of contents finds the headings).
+ */
+final class Blank {
+  private Blank() { }
+
+  static final String W_NS = "xmlns:w=\"" + Xml.W + "\" xmlns:r=\"" + Xml.R + "\"";
+
+  static final String CONTENT_TYPES = "<Types xmlns=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types\">"
+      + "<Default Extension=\"rels\" ContentType=\"application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml\"/>"
+      + "<Default Extension=\"xml\" ContentType=\"application/xml\"/>"
+      + "<Override PartName=\"/word/document.xml\" ContentType=\"application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml\"/>"
+      + "<Override PartName=\"/word/styles.xml\" ContentType=\"application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.styles+xml\"/>"
+      + "<Override PartName=\"/word/settings.xml\" ContentType=\"application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.settings+xml\"/>"
+      + "<Override PartName=\"/word/numbering.xml\" ContentType=\"application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.numbering+xml\"/>"
+      + "<Override PartName=\"/docProps/core.xml\" ContentType=\"application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.core-properties+xml\"/>"
+      + "<Override PartName=\"/docProps/app.xml\" ContentType=\"application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.extended-properties+xml\"/>"
+      + "</Types>";
+
+  static final String RELS = "<Relationships xmlns=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships\">"
+      + "<Relationship Id=\"rId1\" Type=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument\" Target=\"word/document.xml\"/>"
+      + "<Relationship Id=\"rId2\" Type=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships/metadata/core-properties\" Target=\"docProps/core.xml\"/>"
+      + "<Relationship Id=\"rId3\" Type=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/extended-properties\" Target=\"docProps/app.xml\"/>"
+      + "</Relationships>";
+
+  static final String DOC_RELS = "<Relationships xmlns=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships\">"
+      + "<Relationship Id=\"rId1\" Type=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/styles\" Target=\"styles.xml\"/>"
+      + "<Relationship Id=\"rId2\" Type=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/settings\" Target=\"settings.xml\"/>"
+      + "<Relationship Id=\"rId3\" Type=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/numbering\" Target=\"numbering.xml\"/>"
+      + "</Relationships>";
+
+  static final String DOCUMENT = "<w:document " + W_NS + "><w:body><w:p/>"
+      + "<w:sectPr><w:pgSz w:w=\"12240\" w:h=\"15840\"/>"
+      + "<w:pgMar w:top=\"1440\" w:right=\"1440\" w:bottom=\"1440\" w:left=\"1440\" w:header=\"720\" w:footer=\"720\" w:gutter=\"0\"/>"
+      + "<w:cols w:space=\"720\"/><w:docGrid w:linePitch=\"360\"/></w:sectPr></w:body></w:document>";
+
+  static final String SETTINGS = "<w:settings " + W_NS + "><w:zoom w:percent=\"100\"/><w:defaultTabStop w:val=\"720\"/>"
+      + "<w:characterSpacingControl w:val=\"doNotCompress\"/>"
+      + "<w:compat><w:compatSetting w:name=\"compatibilityMode\" w:uri=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/word\" w:val=\"15\"/></w:compat>"
+      + "</w:settings>";
+
+  private static String heading(int n, int size, String color, String before, boolean italic) {
+    return "<w:style w:type=\"paragraph\" w:styleId=\"Heading" + n + "\"><w:name w:val=\"heading " + n + "\"/><w:basedOn w:val=\"Normal\"/>"
+        + "<w:next w:val=\"Normal\"/><w:uiPriority w:val=\"9\"/>" + (n > 1 ? "<w:unhideWhenUsed/>" : "") + "<w:qFormat/>"
+        + "<w:pPr><w:keepNext/><w:keepLines/><w:spacing w:before=\"" + before + "\" w:after=\"0\"/><w:outlineLvl w:val=\"" + (n - 1) + "\"/></w:pPr>"
+        + "<w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii=\"Calibri Light\" w:hAnsi=\"Calibri Light\" w:eastAsia=\"Calibri Light\" w:cs=\"Calibri Light\"/>" + (italic ? "<w:i/><w:iCs/>" : "")
+        + "<w:color w:val=\"" + color + "\"/>" + (size > 0 ? "<w:sz w:val=\"" + size + "\"/><w:szCs w:val=\"" + size + "\"/>" : "") + "</w:rPr></w:style>";
+  }
+
+  static final String STYLES = "<w:styles " + W_NS + ">"
+      + "<w:docDefaults><w:rPrDefault><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii=\"Calibri\" w:eastAsia=\"Calibri\" w:hAnsi=\"Calibri\" w:cs=\"Calibri\"/>"
+      + "<w:sz w:val=\"22\"/><w:szCs w:val=\"22\"/><w:lang w:val=\"en-US\" w:eastAsia=\"en-US\" w:bidi=\"ar-SA\"/></w:rPr></w:rPrDefault>"
+      + "<w:pPrDefault><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after=\"160\" w:line=\"259\" w:lineRule=\"auto\"/></w:pPr></w:pPrDefault></w:docDefaults>"
+      + "<w:style w:type=\"paragraph\" w:default=\"1\" w:styleId=\"Normal\"><w:name w:val=\"Normal\"/><w:qFormat/></w:style>"
+      + heading(1, 32, "2F5496", "240", false)
+      + heading(2, 26, "2F5496", "40", false)
+      + heading(3, 24, "1F3763", "40", false)
+      + heading(4, 0, "2F5496", "40", true)
+      + "<w:style w:type=\"character\" w:default=\"1\" w:styleId=\"DefaultParagraphFont\"><w:name w:val=\"Default Paragraph Font\"/>"
+      + "<w:uiPriority w:val=\"1\"/><w:semiHidden/><w:unhideWhenUsed/></w:style>"
+      + "<w:style w:type=\"table\" w:default=\"1\" w:styleId=\"TableNormal\"><w:name w:val=\"Normal Table\"/><w:uiPriority w:val=\"99\"/>"
+      + "<w:semiHidden/><w:unhideWhenUsed/><w:tblPr><w:tblInd w:w=\"0\" w:type=\"dxa\"/><w:tblCellMar><w:top w:w=\"0\" w:type=\"dxa\"/>"
+      + "<w:left w:w=\"108\" w:type=\"dxa\"/><w:bottom w:w=\"0\" w:type=\"dxa\"/><w:right w:w=\"108\" w:type=\"dxa\"/></w:tblCellMar></w:tblPr></w:style>"
+      + "<w:style w:type=\"numbering\" w:default=\"1\" w:styleId=\"NoList\"><w:name w:val=\"No List\"/><w:uiPriority w:val=\"99\"/>"
+      + "<w:semiHidden/><w:unhideWhenUsed/></w:style>"
+      + "<w:style w:type=\"paragraph\" w:styleId=\"Title\"><w:name w:val=\"Title\"/><w:basedOn w:val=\"Normal\"/><w:next w:val=\"Normal\"/>"
+      + "<w:uiPriority w:val=\"10\"/><w:qFormat/><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after=\"0\" w:line=\"240\" w:lineRule=\"auto\"/><w:contextualSpacing/></w:pPr>"
+      + "<w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii=\"Calibri Light\" w:hAnsi=\"Calibri Light\" w:eastAsia=\"Calibri Light\" w:cs=\"Calibri Light\"/>"
+      + "<w:spacing w:val=\"-10\"/><w:kern w:val=\"28\"/><w:sz w:val=\"56\"/><w:szCs w:val=\"56\"/></w:rPr></w:style>"
+      + "<w:style w:type=\"paragraph\" w:styleId=\"Subtitle\"><w:name w:val=\"Subtitle\"/><w:basedOn w:val=\"Normal\"/><w:next w:val=\"Normal\"/>"
+      + "<w:uiPriority w:val=\"11\"/><w:qFormat/><w:rPr><w:color w:val=\"5A5A5A\"/><w:spacing w:val=\"15\"/></w:rPr></w:style>"
+      + "<w:style w:type=\"paragraph\" w:styleId=\"ListParagraph\"><w:name w:val=\"List Paragraph\"/><w:basedOn w:val=\"Normal\"/>"
+      + "<w:uiPriority w:val=\"34\"/><w:qFormat/><w:pPr><w:ind w:left=\"720\"/><w:contextualSpacing/></w:pPr></w:style>"
+      + "<w:style w:type=\"paragraph\" w:styleId=\"NoSpacing\"><w:name w:val=\"No Spacing\"/><w:uiPriority w:val=\"1\"/><w:qFormat/>"
+      + "<w:pPr><w:spacing w:after=\"0\" w:line=\"240\" w:lineRule=\"auto\"/></w:pPr></w:style>"
+      + "<w:style w:type=\"character\" w:styleId=\"Hyperlink\"><w:name w:val=\"Hyperlink\"/><w:basedOn w:val=\"DefaultParagraphFont\"/>"
+      + "<w:uiPriority w:val=\"99\"/><w:unhideWhenUsed/><w:rPr><w:color w:val=\"0563C1\"/><w:u w:val=\"single\"/></w:rPr></w:style>"
+      + "<w:style w:type=\"table\" w:styleId=\"TableGrid\"><w:name w:val=\"Table Grid\"/><w:basedOn w:val=\"TableNormal\"/><w:uiPriority w:val=\"39\"/>"
+      + "<w:pPr><w:spacing w:after=\"0\" w:line=\"240\" w:lineRule=\"auto\"/></w:pPr><w:tblPr><w:tblBorders>"
+      + "<w:top w:val=\"single\" w:sz=\"4\" w:space=\"0\" w:color=\"auto\"/><w:left w:val=\"single\" w:sz=\"4\" w:space=\"0\" w:color=\"auto\"/>"
+      + "<w:bottom w:val=\"single\" w:sz=\"4\" w:space=\"0\" w:color=\"auto\"/><w:right w:val=\"single\" w:sz=\"4\" w:space=\"0\" w:color=\"auto\"/>"
+      + "<w:insideH w:val=\"single\" w:sz=\"4\" w:space=\"0\" w:color=\"auto\"/><w:insideV w:val=\"single\" w:sz=\"4\" w:space=\"0\" w:color=\"auto\"/>"
+      + "</w:tblBorders></w:tblPr></w:style>"
+      + "</w:styles>";
+
+  /** A list definition: bullet (• o ▪ by level) or numbered (1. a. i. by level), nine levels. */
+  static String abstractNum(int id, boolean bullet) {
+    StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
+    b.append("<w:abstractNum ").append(W_NS).append(" w:abstractNumId=\"").append(id).append("\"><w:multiLevelType w:val=\"hybridMultilevel\"/>");
+    String[] bullets = { "•", "o", "▪" };
+    String[] fmts = { "decimal", "lowerLetter", "lowerRoman" };
+    for (int l = 0; l < 9; l++) {
+      b.append("<w:lvl w:ilvl=\"").append(l).append("\"><w:start w:val=\"1\"/>");
+      if (bullet) b.append("<w:numFmt w:val=\"bullet\"/><w:lvlText w:val=\"").append(bullets[l % 3]).append("\"/>");
+      else b.append("<w:numFmt w:val=\"").append(fmts[l % 3]).append("\"/><w:lvlText w:val=\"%").append(l + 1).append(".\"/>");
+      b.append("<w:lvlJc w:val=\"").append(!bullet && l % 3 == 2 ? "right" : "left").append("\"/>");
+      b.append("<w:pPr><w:ind w:left=\"").append(720 * (l + 1)).append("\" w:hanging=\"").append(!bullet && l % 3 == 2 ? 180 : 360).append("\"/></w:pPr>");
+      if (bullet && l % 3 == 1) b.append("<w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii=\"Courier New\" w:hAnsi=\"Courier New\" w:cs=\"Courier New\" w:hint=\"default\"/></w:rPr>");
+      b.append("</w:lvl>");
+    }
+    b.append("</w:abstractNum>");
+    return b.toString();
+  }
+
+  static String numbering() {
+    String a0 = abstractNum(0, true).replace(" " + W_NS, "");
+    String a1 = abstractNum(1, false).replace(" " + W_NS, "");
+    return "<w:numbering " + W_NS + ">" + a0 + a1
+        + "<w:num w:numId=\"1\"><w:abstractNumId w:val=\"0\"/></w:num><w:num w:numId=\"2\"><w:abstractNumId w:val=\"1\"/></w:num></w:numbering>";
+  }
+
+  static final String CORE = "<cp:coreProperties xmlns:cp=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/metadata/core-properties\" "
+      + "xmlns:dc=\"http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/\" xmlns:dcterms=\"http://purl.org/dc/terms/\" xmlns:dcmitype=\"http://purl.org/dc/dcmitype/\" "
+      + "xmlns:xsi=\"http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance\"><dc:title></dc:title><dc:creator></dc:creator>"
+      + "<dcterms:created xsi:type=\"dcterms:W3CDTF\">@NOW@</dcterms:created><dcterms:modified xsi:type=\"dcterms:W3CDTF\">@NOW@</dcterms:modified>"
+      + "</cp:coreProperties>";
+
+  static final String APP = "<Properties xmlns=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/extended-properties\" "
+      + "xmlns:vt=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/docPropsVTypes\"><Application>code_boss</Application></Properties>";
+
+  static byte[] bytes() throws Exception {
+    String now = java.time.Instant.now().truncatedTo(java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit.SECONDS).toString();
+    String[][] parts = {
+        { "[Content_Types].xml", CONTENT_TYPES }, { "_rels/.rels", RELS }, { "word/document.xml", DOCUMENT },
+        { "word/_rels/document.xml.rels", DOC_RELS }, { "word/styles.xml", STYLES }, { "word/settings.xml", SETTINGS },
+        { "word/numbering.xml", numbering() }, { "docProps/core.xml", CORE.replace("@NOW@", now) }, { "docProps/app.xml", APP } };
+    ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
+    ZipOutputStream z = new ZipOutputStream(bo);
+    for (String[] p : parts) {
+      z.putNextEntry(new ZipEntry(p[0]));
+      z.write(("<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"UTF-8\" standalone=\"yes\"?>\r\n" + p[1]).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
+      z.closeEntry();
+    }
+    z.close();
+    return bo.toByteArray();
+  }
+}
+

=== add docx-helper/src/main/java/com/codeboss/docx/DocxHelper.java

+package com.codeboss.docx;
+
+import java.io.BufferedReader;
+import java.io.InputStreamReader;
+import java.io.PrintStream;
+import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
+import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
+import java.util.Map;
+
+/**
+ * code_boss's Word (.docx) support: read, search and edit, with nothing but the JDK (zip + XML).
+ *
+ *   java -cp <classes> com.codeboss.docx.DocxHelper read|search|edit     one request (JSON) on stdin, one answer on stdout
+ *   java -cp <classes> com.codeboss.docx.DocxHelper serve [idle minutes]  one request per line, answered in order
+ *
+ * SERVE is the same pattern as git's service in the container (cb.jgit.Serve): one JVM that stays up, because a JVM's
+ * start is most of a one-shot call. A request line is {"id":..,"op":"read"|"search"|"edit",...the op's fields}; the
+ * answer line is {"id":..,...the result}. It ends when stdin closes (the node that started it is gone) or after the
+ * idle minutes. A document is read whole and closed before the answer: no file stays open between requests.
+ *
+ * READ   {docx, format?, from?, to?, ids?}       -> {ok, paragraphs:[{id, text, style, heading, list, ...}], summary}
+ * SEARCH {docx, query, regex?, caseSensitive?}   -> {ok, paragraphs:[...+matches], matches}
+ * EDIT   {docx, ops:[...], tracked?, author?, dryRun?}  -> {ok, written, changes:[{id, before, after}], ...}
+ * CREATE {docx, template?, empty?, content?:[...], overwrite?}  -> {ok, created, changes?}
+ * ERROR  {ok:false, code, error, op?}
+ */
+public final class DocxHelper {
+  private DocxHelper() { }
+
+  static final String VERSION = "3";
+
+  public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
+    PrintStream out = new PrintStream(System.out, true, "UTF-8");
+    String op = args.length > 0 ? args[0] : "";
+    if ("serve".equals(op)) { serve(out, args.length > 1 ? Long.parseLong(args[1]) : 15); return; }
+    if ("version".equals(op)) { out.println("{\"ok\":true,\"version\":\"" + VERSION + "\"}"); return; }
+    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
+    BufferedReader in = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
+    char[] buf = new char[1 << 15];
+    int n;
+    while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) sb.append(buf, 0, n);
+    Map<String, Object> res;
+    try { res = handle(op, Json.obj(Json.parse(sb.toString()))); }
+    catch (RuntimeException e) { res = error(new Fail("bad-request", "the request is not valid JSON: " + e.getMessage())); }
+    out.println(Json.write(res));
+    System.exit(Boolean.TRUE.equals(res.get("ok")) ? 0 : 2);
+  }
+
+  static volatile long last = System.currentTimeMillis();
+  static volatile boolean busy;
+
+  private static void serve(PrintStream out, long idleMinutes) throws Exception {
+    final long idleMs = Math.max(1, idleMinutes) * 60000L;
+    Thread idle = new Thread(new Runnable() {
+      public void run() {
+        for (;;) {
+          try { Thread.sleep(15000); } catch (InterruptedException e) { return; }
+          if (!busy && System.currentTimeMillis() - last > idleMs) System.exit(0);
+        }
+      }
+    }, "cb-docx-idle");
+    idle.setDaemon(true);
+    idle.start();
+    BufferedReader in = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
+    out.println("{\"ready\":true,\"version\":\"" + VERSION + "\"}");
+    for (;;) {
+      String line = in.readLine();
+      if (line == null) System.exit(0);   // the node that started us is gone
+      if (line.trim().isEmpty()) continue;
+      busy = true;
+      Object id = null;
+      Map<String, Object> res;
+      try {
+        Map<String, Object> req = Json.obj(Json.parse(line));
+        id = req.get("id");
+        res = handle(Json.str(req.get("op")), req);
+      } catch (RuntimeException e) {
+        res = error(new Fail("bad-request", "the request is not valid JSON: " + e.getMessage()));
+      }
+      Map<String, Object> o = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+      o.put("id", id);
+      o.putAll(res);
+      out.println(Json.write(o));
+      last = System.currentTimeMillis();
+      busy = false;
+    }
+  }
+
+  static Map<String, Object> handle(String op, Map<String, Object> spec) {
+    try {
+      if ("version".equals(op)) { Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>(); m.put("ok", Boolean.TRUE); m.put("version", VERSION); return m; }
+      if ("create".equals(op)) return Doc.create(spec);
+      Doc d = Doc.open(Json.str(spec.get("docx")));
+      if ("read".equals(op)) return d.read(spec);
+      if ("search".equals(op)) return d.search(spec);
+      if ("edit".equals(op)) return d.edit(spec);
+      throw new Fail("bad-request", "unknown op \"" + op + "\" (read, search, edit, create)");
+    } catch (Fail f) {
+      return error(f);
+    } catch (OutOfMemoryError e) {
+      return error(new Fail("too-big", "the document is too big for the helper's memory"));
+    } catch (Throwable t) {
+      StackTraceElement[] st = t.getStackTrace();
+      String where = st.length > 0 ? " at " + st[0] : "";
+      return error(new Fail("internal", "nothing was written: " + t + where + " - this is a bug in code_boss's Word support; please report it"));
+    }
+  }
+
+  static Map<String, Object> error(Fail f) {
+    Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+    m.put("ok", Boolean.FALSE);
+    m.put("code", f.code);
+    m.put("error", f.getMessage());
+    if (f.op != null) m.put("op", (long) f.op.intValue());
+    return m;
+  }
+}
+

+package com.codeboss.docx;
+
+/** A refusal or an error with a code the caller can act on; nothing has been written when one is thrown. */
+final class Fail extends Exception {
+  private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;
+  final String code;
+  Integer op;   // which op (0-based) it is about, when it is about one
+
+  Fail(String code, String message) { super(message); this.code = code; }
+
+  Fail at(int opIndex) { if (op == null) op = Integer.valueOf(opIndex); return this; }
+}

+package com.codeboss.docx;
+
+import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
+import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.io.InputStream;
+import java.nio.file.AtomicMoveNotSupportedException;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.nio.file.Path;
+import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
+import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+import java.util.Set;
+import java.util.zip.CRC32;
+import java.util.zip.ZipEntry;
+import java.util.zip.ZipInputStream;
+import java.util.zip.ZipOutputStream;
+
+import org.w3c.dom.Document;
+import org.w3c.dom.Element;
+
+/**
+ * A .docx as its zip entries. Only the parts an edit changed are written again; every other entry is copied byte for
+ * byte, so nothing we did not mean to touch can change (images, embedded files, macros, custom XML, parts we do not
+ * understand). The file is read whole and closed at once: the helper never holds a document open.
+ */
+final class Pkg {
+  static final class Entry {
+    final String name;
+    final byte[] bytes;
+    final int method;
+    final long time;
+    Entry(String name, byte[] bytes, int method, long time) { this.name = name; this.bytes = bytes; this.method = method; this.time = time; }
+  }
+
+  final List<Entry> entries = new ArrayList<Entry>();
+  final Map<String, Entry> byName = new LinkedHashMap<String, Entry>();
+  private final Map<String, Document> doms = new LinkedHashMap<String, Document>();
+  final Set<String> dirty = new LinkedHashSet<String>();
+  String mainPart = "word/document.xml";
+
+  static Pkg load(byte[] bytes) throws IOException {
+    Pkg p = new Pkg();
+    ZipInputStream z = new ZipInputStream(new ByteArrayInputStream(bytes));
+    try {
+      ZipEntry e;
+      while ((e = z.getNextEntry()) != null) {
+        if (e.isDirectory()) continue;
+        byte[] b = readAll(z);
+        Entry en = new Entry(e.getName(), b, e.getMethod() == ZipEntry.STORED ? ZipEntry.STORED : ZipEntry.DEFLATED, e.getTime());
+        if (p.byName.containsKey(en.name)) throw new IOException("the zip holds " + en.name + " twice");
+        p.entries.add(en);
+        p.byName.put(en.name, en);
+      }
+    } finally { z.close(); }
+    if (!p.byName.containsKey("[Content_Types].xml")) throw new IOException("not a Word document (no [Content_Types].xml)");
+    // the main part, from the package relationships (word/document.xml unless something unusual made it)
+    try {
+      Document rels = p.dom("_rels/.rels");
+      for (Element r : relationships(rels)) {
+        if (r.getAttribute("Type").endsWith("/officeDocument")) { p.mainPart = resolve("", r.getAttribute("Target")); break; }
+      }
+    } catch (Exception ignore) { }
+    if (!p.byName.containsKey(p.mainPart)) throw new IOException("not a Word document (no " + p.mainPart + ")");
+    return p;
+  }
+
+  boolean has(String part) { return byName.containsKey(part); }
+
+  Document dom(String part) throws Exception {
+    Document d = doms.get(part);
+    if (d != null) return d;
+    Entry e = byName.get(part);
+    if (e == null) return null;
+    d = Xml.parse(e.bytes);
+    doms.put(part, d);
+    return d;
+  }
+
+  void touch(String part) { dirty.add(part); }
+
+  static final String CT_NS = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types";
+
+  /** A new part (written from its DOM), with its content type. */
+  void add(String part, Document d, String contentType) throws Exception {
+    if (byName.containsKey(part)) throw new IOException(part + " is already in the package");
+    Entry e = new Entry(part, new byte[0], ZipEntry.DEFLATED, System.currentTimeMillis());
+    entries.add(e);
+    byName.put(part, e);
+    doms.put(part, d);
+    dirty.add(part);
+    setContentType(part, contentType);
+  }
+
+  /** The Override for one part in [Content_Types].xml, added or changed. */
+  void setContentType(String part, String contentType) throws Exception {
+    Document ct = dom("[Content_Types].xml");
+    Element root = ct.getDocumentElement();
+    String name = "/" + part;
+    org.w3c.dom.NodeList nl = root.getElementsByTagNameNS(CT_NS, "Override");
+    for (int i = 0; i < nl.getLength(); i++) {
+      Element o = (Element) nl.item(i);
+      if (name.equalsIgnoreCase(o.getAttribute("PartName"))) {
+        if (!contentType.equals(o.getAttribute("ContentType"))) { o.setAttribute("ContentType", contentType); touch("[Content_Types].xml"); }
+        return;
+      }
+    }
+    Element o = ct.createElementNS(CT_NS, root.getPrefix() == null ? "Override" : root.getPrefix() + ":Override");
+    o.setAttribute("PartName", name);
+    o.setAttribute("ContentType", contentType);
+    root.appendChild(o);
+    touch("[Content_Types].xml");
+  }
+
+  /** The content type a part has (its Override, else its extension's Default). */
+  String contentType(String part) throws Exception {
+    Element root = dom("[Content_Types].xml").getDocumentElement();
+    org.w3c.dom.NodeList nl = root.getElementsByTagNameNS(CT_NS, "Override");
+    for (int i = 0; i < nl.getLength(); i++) { Element o = (Element) nl.item(i); if (("/" + part).equalsIgnoreCase(o.getAttribute("PartName"))) return o.getAttribute("ContentType"); }
+    String ext = part.substring(part.lastIndexOf('.') + 1);
+    nl = root.getElementsByTagNameNS(CT_NS, "Default");
+    for (int i = 0; i < nl.getLength(); i++) { Element o = (Element) nl.item(i); if (ext.equalsIgnoreCase(o.getAttribute("Extension"))) return o.getAttribute("ContentType"); }
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  /** A relationship from a part to another part of the package; returns its Id. */
+  String addRel(String part, String type, String target) throws Exception {
+    int slash = part.lastIndexOf('/');
+    String dir = slash < 0 ? "" : part.substring(0, slash + 1);
+    String relPart = dir + "_rels/" + part.substring(slash + 1) + ".rels";
+    Document d = dom(relPart);
+    if (d == null) throw new IOException(part + " has no relationships part");
+    Element root = d.getDocumentElement();
+    Set<String> ids = new java.util.HashSet<String>();
+    for (Element r : relationships(d)) ids.add(r.getAttribute("Id"));
+    int n = ids.size() + 1;
+    while (ids.contains("rId" + n)) n++;
+    Element r = d.createElementNS(Xml.PKG_REL, root.getPrefix() == null ? "Relationship" : root.getPrefix() + ":Relationship");
+    r.setAttribute("Id", "rId" + n);
+    r.setAttribute("Type", type);
+    r.setAttribute("Target", target);
+    root.appendChild(r);
+    touch(relPart);
+    return "rId" + n;
+  }
+
+  /** The relationships of a part: Id -> {Type, Target(resolved), TargetMode}. */
+  Map<String, String[]> rels(String part) throws Exception {
+    Map<String, String[]> out = new LinkedHashMap<String, String[]>();
+    int slash = part.lastIndexOf('/');
+    String dir = slash < 0 ? "" : part.substring(0, slash + 1);
+    String relPart = dir + "_rels/" + part.substring(slash + 1) + ".rels";
+    Document d = dom(relPart);
+    if (d == null) return out;
+    for (Element r : relationships(d)) {
+      String mode = r.getAttribute("TargetMode");
+      String target = "External".equals(mode) ? r.getAttribute("Target") : resolve(dir, r.getAttribute("Target"));
+      out.put(r.getAttribute("Id"), new String[] { r.getAttribute("Type"), target, mode });
+    }
+    return out;
+  }
+
+  private static List<Element> relationships(Document d) {
+    List<Element> out = new ArrayList<Element>();
+    org.w3c.dom.NodeList nl = d.getElementsByTagNameNS(Xml.PKG_REL, "Relationship");
+    for (int i = 0; i < nl.getLength(); i++) out.add((Element) nl.item(i));
+    return out;
+  }
+
+  static String resolve(String dir, String target) {
+    String t = target.startsWith("/") ? target.substring(1) : dir + target;
+    List<String> parts = new ArrayList<String>();
+    for (String s : t.split("/")) {
+      if (s.isEmpty() || s.equals(".")) continue;
+      if (s.equals("..")) { if (!parts.isEmpty()) parts.remove(parts.size() - 1); continue; }
+      parts.add(s);
+    }
+    return String.join("/", parts);
+  }
+
+  /** The package as bytes: changed parts written again, the rest exactly as they came. */
+  byte[] bytes() throws IOException {
+    ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream(1 << 16);
+    ZipOutputStream z = new ZipOutputStream(bo);
+    for (Entry e : entries) {
+      byte[] b = e.bytes;
+      if (dirty.contains(e.name)) b = Xml.write(doms.get(e.name));
+      ZipEntry ze = new ZipEntry(e.name);
+      if (e.time > 0) ze.setTime(e.time);
+      if (e.method == ZipEntry.STORED) {
+        ze.setMethod(ZipEntry.STORED);
+        ze.setSize(b.length);
+        ze.setCompressedSize(b.length);
+        CRC32 c = new CRC32(); c.update(b); ze.setCrc(c.getValue());
+      } else ze.setMethod(ZipEntry.DEFLATED);
+      z.putNextEntry(ze);
+      z.write(b);
+      z.closeEntry();
+    }
+    z.close();
+    return bo.toByteArray();
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Replace the file with bytes: a sibling temp file, then a move over the original (retried - on Windows a scanner or
+   * an indexer holding the file for a moment makes one rename fail). A file Word has open cannot be replaced; the
+   * error says so instead of "access denied".
+   */
+  static void replaceFile(Path path, byte[] bytes) throws Exception {
+    Path tmp = path.resolveSibling(path.getFileName().toString() + ".cb-" + Long.toHexString(System.nanoTime()) + ".tmp");
+    Files.write(tmp, bytes);
+    Exception last = null;
+    try {
+      for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
+        try {
+          try { Files.move(tmp, path, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE); }
+          catch (AtomicMoveNotSupportedException e) { Files.move(tmp, path, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING); }
+          return;
+        } catch (IOException e) {
+          last = e;
+          try { Thread.sleep(100); } catch (InterruptedException ie) { break; }
+        }
+      }
+    } finally {
+      try { Files.deleteIfExists(tmp); } catch (Exception ignore) { }
+    }
+    if (openInWord(path)) throw new Fail("file-open", "the document is open in Word - close it there, then edit it again");
+    throw new Fail("file-error", "could not replace " + path + ": " + (last == null ? "?" : last.getMessage()));
+  }
+
+  /** Word keeps an owner file (~$ + the name less its first two characters) beside a document it has open. */
+  static boolean openInWord(Path path) {
+    String n = path.getFileName().toString();
+    String owner = "~$" + (n.length() > 2 ? n.substring(2) : n);
+    return Files.exists(path.resolveSibling(owner));
+  }
+
+  static byte[] readAll(InputStream in) throws IOException {
+    ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
+    byte[] buf = new byte[1 << 15];
+    int n;
+    while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) bo.write(buf, 0, n);
+    return bo.toByteArray();
+  }
+}

=== add docx-helper/src/main/java/com/codeboss/docx/Xml.java
new 6738435bb2381665740a32c5
+package com.codeboss.docx;
+
+import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
+import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.List;
+
+import javax.xml.parsers.DocumentBuilder;
+import javax.xml.parsers.DocumentBuilderFactory;
+
+import org.w3c.dom.Attr;
+import org.w3c.dom.Document;
+import org.w3c.dom.Element;
+import org.w3c.dom.NamedNodeMap;
+import org.w3c.dom.Node;
+
+/**
+ * XML for the parts of a .docx: the JDK's DOM to read, and our own writer to write. Our own writer because the JDK's
+ * serializer has had bugs with characters outside the BMP (an emoji written as two invalid character references), and
+ * because what it writes must be exactly what we compare against when we check a write.
+ */
+final class Xml {
+  private Xml() { }
+
+  static final String W = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main";
+  static final String R = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships";
+  static final String M = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/math";
+  static final String W14 = "http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/word/2010/wordml";
+  static final String WP = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/wordprocessingDrawing";
+  static final String XML_NS = "http://www.w3.org/XML/1998/namespace";
+  static final String PKG_REL = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships";
+
+  static Document parse(byte[] bytes) throws Exception {
+    DocumentBuilderFactory f = DocumentBuilderFactory.newInstance();
+    f.setNamespaceAware(true);
+    f.setCoalescing(true);
+    f.setExpandEntityReferences(false);
+    try { f.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/disallow-doctype-decl", true); } catch (Exception ignore) { }
+    try { f.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-general-entities", false); } catch (Exception ignore) { }
+    try { f.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-parameter-entities", false); } catch (Exception ignore) { }
+    DocumentBuilder b = f.newDocumentBuilder();
+    b.setErrorHandler(null);
+    return b.parse(new ByteArrayInputStream(bytes));
+  }
+
+  /** The whole part, with the declaration Word writes. */
+  static byte[] write(Document d) {
+    StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(1 << 16);
+    b.append("<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"UTF-8\" standalone=\"yes\"?>\r\n");
+    for (Node n = d.getFirstChild(); n != null; n = n.getNextSibling()) node(b, n);
+    return b.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
+  }
+
+  /** One node and what is under it - what a check compares. */
+  static String str(Node n) {
+    StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(256);
+    node(b, n);
+    return b.toString();
+  }
+
+  private static void node(StringBuilder b, Node n) {
+    switch (n.getNodeType()) {
+      case Node.ELEMENT_NODE: {
+        b.append('<').append(n.getNodeName());
+        NamedNodeMap as = n.getAttributes();
+        for (int i = 0; i < as.getLength(); i++) {
+          Attr a = (Attr) as.item(i);
+          b.append(' ').append(a.getName()).append("=\"");
+          esc(b, a.getValue(), true);
+          b.append('"');
+        }
+        Node c = n.getFirstChild();
+        if (c == null) { b.append("/>"); return; }
+        b.append('>');
+        for (; c != null; c = c.getNextSibling()) node(b, c);
+        b.append("</").append(n.getNodeName()).append('>');
+        return;
+      }
+      case Node.TEXT_NODE: esc(b, n.getNodeValue(), false); return;
+      case Node.CDATA_SECTION_NODE: esc(b, n.getNodeValue(), false); return;
+      case Node.PROCESSING_INSTRUCTION_NODE: b.append("<?").append(n.getNodeName()).append(' ').append(n.getNodeValue()).append("?>"); return;
+      case Node.COMMENT_NODE: b.append("<!--").append(n.getNodeValue()).append("-->"); return;
+      default: return;
+    }
+  }
+
+  private static void esc(StringBuilder b, String s, boolean attr) {
+    if (s == null) return;
+    for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
+      char c = s.charAt(i);
+      switch (c) {
+        case '&': b.append("&amp;"); break;
+        case '<': b.append("&lt;"); break;
+        case '>': b.append("&gt;"); break;
+        case '"': if (attr) b.append("&quot;"); else b.append(c); break;
+        case '\r': b.append("&#13;"); break;
+        case '\n': if (attr) b.append("&#10;"); else b.append(c); break;
+        case '\t': if (attr) b.append("&#9;"); else b.append(c); break;
+        default: b.append(c);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  // ---- small DOM helpers (WordprocessingML elements) ----
+
+  static boolean isW(Node n, String local) {
+    return n != null && n.getNodeType() == Node.ELEMENT_NODE && W.equals(n.getNamespaceURI()) && local.equals(n.getLocalName());
+  }
+
+  static Element child(Node parent, String local) {
+    if (parent == null) return null;
+    for (Node c = parent.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNextSibling()) if (isW(c, local)) return (Element) c;
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  static List<Element> children(Node parent) {
+    List<Element> out = new ArrayList<Element>();
+    if (parent == null) return out;
+    for (Node c = parent.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNextSibling()) if (c.getNodeType() == Node.ELEMENT_NODE) out.add((Element) c);
+    return out;
+  }
+
+  static List<Element> childrenW(Node parent, String local) {
+    List<Element> out = new ArrayList<Element>();
+    if (parent == null) return out;
+    for (Node c = parent.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNextSibling()) if (isW(c, local)) out.add((Element) c);
+    return out;
+  }
+
+  /** w:val (or another w: attribute) of an element; null when absent. */
+  static String wattr(Element e, String local) {
+    if (e == null) return null;
+    String v = e.getAttributeNS(W, local);
+    return (v == null || v.isEmpty()) && !e.hasAttributeNS(W, local) ? null : v;
+  }
+
+  static String val(Element parent, String child) { return wattr(child(parent, child), "val"); }
+
+  /** The prefix this document uses for WordprocessingML (always "w" from Word, but not promised). */
+  static String wPrefix(Document d) {
+    Element root = d.getDocumentElement();
+    String p = root == null ? null : root.lookupPrefix(W);
+    return p == null ? "w" : p;
+  }
+
+  static Element el(Document d, String local) { return d.createElementNS(W, wPrefix(d) + ":" + local); }
+
+  static void setW(Element e, String local, String v) { e.setAttributeNS(W, wPrefix(e.getOwnerDocument()) + ":" + local, v); }
+
+  static void removeChildren(Node n) { while (n.getFirstChild() != null) n.removeChild(n.getFirstChild()); }
+
+  /** Every text of w:t under a node (what a run or a block shows), tabs and breaks as \t and \n. */
+  static String plainText(Node n) {
+    StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
+    plain(b, n);
+    return b.toString();
+  }
+
+  private static void plain(StringBuilder b, Node n) {
+    for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNextSibling()) {
+      if (c.getNodeType() != Node.ELEMENT_NODE) continue;
+      if (isW(c, "t")) b.append(c.getTextContent());
+      else if (isW(c, "tab")) b.append('\t');
+      else if (isW(c, "br") || isW(c, "cr")) b.append('\n');
+      else if (isW(c, "p")) { if (b.length() > 0) b.append('\n'); plain(b, c); }
+      else if (!isW(c, "instrText") && !isW(c, "delText") && !isW(c, "del")) plain(b, c);
+    }
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Insert child into parent at the place the schema's sequence gives it (order lists a parent's children by local
+   * name). An existing child of the same name is replaced. Children the list does not know keep their place; the new
+   * one goes before the first known child that must follow it.
+   */
+  static void putInOrder(Element parent, Element child, String[] order) {
+    String name = child.getLocalName();
+    Element old = child(parent, name);
+    if (old != null) { parent.replaceChild(child, old); return; }
+    int rank = indexOf(order, name);
+    for (Node c = parent.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNextSibling()) {
+      if (c.getNodeType() != Node.ELEMENT_NODE || !W.equals(c.getNamespaceURI())) continue;
+      int r = indexOf(order, c.getLocalName());
+      if (r > rank) { parent.insertBefore(child, c); return; }
+    }
+    parent.appendChild(child);
+  }
+
+  static int indexOf(String[] a, String s) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if (a[i].equals(s)) return i;
+    return a.length;   // unknown names sort last
+  }
+
+  /** CT_RPr's sequence. */
+  static final String[] RPR_ORDER = { "rStyle", "rFonts", "b", "bCs", "i", "iCs", "caps", "smallCaps", "strike", "dstrike", "outline",
+      "shadow", "emboss", "imprint", "noProof", "snapToGrid", "vanish", "webHidden", "color", "spacing", "w", "kern", "position", "sz",

+      "suppressLineNumbers", "pBdr", "shd", "tabs", "suppressAutoHyphens", "kinsoku", "wordWrap", "overflowPunct", "topLinePunct",

+      "suppressOverlap", "jc", "textDirection", "textAlignment", "textboxTightWrap", "outlineLvl", "divId", "cnfStyle", "rPr", "sectPr",
+      "pPrChange" };

+  static final String[] PARA_RPR_ORDER;
+  static {

+    PARA_RPR_ORDER = new String[head.length + RPR_ORDER.length];
+    System.arraycopy(head, 0, PARA_RPR_ORDER, 0, head.length);
+    System.arraycopy(RPR_ORDER, 0, PARA_RPR_ORDER, head.length, RPR_ORDER.length);
+  }

=== add sandbox-launcher/src/main/java/cb/sandbox/Doctor.java
new bd51bec8fc7b0e9fbd50750e
+package cb.sandbox;
+
+import java.io.BufferedReader;
+import java.io.File;
+import java.io.InputStreamReader;
+import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
+import java.nio.file.Files;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+import java.util.concurrent.LinkedBlockingQueue;
+import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
+
+/**
+ * THE CONTAINER'S DOCTOR (the developer, 2026-11-17: "create something similar [to the Inspector] but that sits at the
+ * container level ... it runs checks on things that might stop the LLM from working ... asks for a key if there isn't
+ * one, then it's a chat w/ an agent that can see codeboss instances and logs"; on where: node from the image, OUTSIDE
+ * the container). It is code_boss's own bundle run as `doctor` (src/doctor.mjs) with the image's node, started here on
+ * demand - the page's Troubleshoot button, the tray's menu - and shown in the same kind of window as this page. It runs
+ * outside the container so it works when what is broken is the container, the bridge into it, or the key store; it ends
+ * by itself when it has not been used for a while or this manager is gone, and when this manager quits.
+ */
+final class Doctor {
+  private Doctor() { }
+
+  private static final Object LOCK = new Object();
+  private static Process proc;
+  private static volatile String url = "";
+
+  /** Start it if it is not running, and open its window (window false: only start it). {ok, url} | {ok:false, error} */
+  static Map<String, Object> open(boolean window) {
+    String u;
+    try { u = ensure(); }
+    catch (Exception e) { return Manager.err("The doctor did not start: " + e.getMessage()); }
+    if (window) Manager.Tray.open(u);
+    Map<String, Object> m = Manager.ok();
+    m.put("url", u);
+    return m;
+  }
+
+  static String ensure() throws Exception {
+    synchronized (LOCK) {
+      if (proc != null && proc.isAlive() && !url.isEmpty()) return url;
+      if (!App.inImage()) throw new IllegalStateException("this image has no code_boss in it (tools\\app\\code-boss.mjs), and the doctor is part of it");
+      if (!Files.isRegularFile(App.node())) throw new IllegalStateException("this image has no node (tools\\node\\node.exe) to run it with");
+      File cbDir = Secure.root().getParent().toFile();
+      List<String> cmd = new ArrayList<String>();
+      cmd.add(App.node().toString());
+      cmd.add(App.bundle().toString());
+      cmd.add("doctor");
+      cmd.add("--tree"); cmd.add(Container.tree.toString());
+      cmd.add("--manager"); cmd.add(Manager.pageUrl);
+      cmd.add("--secure"); cmd.add(Secure.root().toString());
+      cmd.add("--cb-dir"); cmd.add(cbDir.toString());
+      ProcessBuilder pb = new ProcessBuilder(cmd);
+      pb.directory(Container.tree.toFile());
+      // it is not code_boss inside the container: none of what tells code_boss it is
+      for (String k : new String[] { "CB_IN_CONTAINER", "CB_CONTAINER_API", "CB_CONTAINER_TOKEN", "CB_HOME", "CB_INSTANCE_ID", "CB_RESTART_KEY" }) pb.environment().remove(k);
+      cbDir.mkdirs();
+      final File err = new File(cbDir, "doctor-out.txt");
+      pb.redirectError(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.appendTo(err));
+      pb.redirectInput(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.from(new File("NUL")));
+      final Process p = pb.start();
+      final LinkedBlockingQueue<String> lines = new LinkedBlockingQueue<String>();
+      Thread reader = new Thread(new Runnable() {
+        public void run() {
+          try (BufferedReader r = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(p.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
+            for (String l; (l = r.readLine()) != null; ) lines.offer(l);   // (read to the end: a full pipe would hold it)
+          } catch (Exception ignore) { }
+        }
+      }, "cb-doctor-out");
+      reader.setDaemon(true);
+      reader.start();
+      long until = System.currentTimeMillis() + 60000L;
+      String found = null;
+      while (found == null && System.currentTimeMillis() < until) {
+        String l = lines.poll(500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
+        if (l != null && l.startsWith("DOCTOR http://127.0.0.1:")) found = l.substring(7).trim();
+        else if (l == null && !p.isAlive()) break;
+      }
+      if (found == null) {
+        p.destroyForcibly();
+        String tail = "";
+        try { tail = Server.tail(new String(Files.readAllBytes(err.toPath()), StandardCharsets.UTF_8), 6); } catch (Exception ignore) { }
+        throw new IllegalStateException(p.isAlive() ? "it did not say where it is within a minute" : "it ended at once" + (tail.isEmpty() ? "" : ":\n" + tail));
+      }
+      proc = p;
+      url = found;
+      return url;
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** This manager quits: so does the doctor. */
+  static void stop() {
+    synchronized (LOCK) {
+      try { if (proc != null && proc.isAlive()) proc.destroy(); } catch (Throwable ignore) { }
+      proc = null;
+      url = "";
+    }
+  }
+}

+    // THE COOKIES A PANEL PAGE'S RESPONSE SETS, KEPT SO THAT THEY COME BACK (prod, 2026-11-16: in the panel, WebLogic's
+    // JSESSIONID never came back and every sign-in failed with 403). A tab's page sits in an iframe on another site than
+    // code_boss's window, so to Chrome it is a third party there, and a cookie without SameSite=None is not even kept -
+    // Lax by default, Lax and Strict alike. A paused response does not show its Set-Cookie, and one changed there is not
+    // taken (measured); its raw headers come first (Network.responseReceivedExtraInfo), with the cookies Chrome blocked
+    // and why. While the response waits, each one blocked for its SameSite is set again (Network.setCookie) as
+    // SameSite=None and Secure - which Chrome allows on http://localhost and 127.0.0.1, both secure to it - and the page
+    // goes on only then. The Node broker (src/agent/panel-broker.mjs) does the same: panelCookieParams, cookiesBack.
+    static final long COOKIE_WAIT_MS = 50;   // (measured: the raw headers come before the pause; a moment, in case)
+    /** This connection's input, one call after another (see the reader loop). */
+    private final ExecutorService inputLine = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r -> { Thread t = new Thread(r, "cb-panel-input"); t.setDaemon(true); return t; });
+    private final java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<String, Object[]> cookieSeen = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<String, Object[]>();
+
+    void cookieNoted(String nid, Map<String, Object> p) {
+      long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
+      cookieSeen.put(nid, new Object[] { Json.array(p.get("blockedCookies")), Long.valueOf(now) });
+      if (cookieSeen.size() > 500) for (Map.Entry<String, Object[]> e : cookieSeen.entrySet()) if (now - ((Long) e.getValue()[1]).longValue() > 30000) cookieSeen.remove(e.getKey());
+    }
+
+    private void cookiesBack(String s, Map<String, Object> p) {
+      String nid = Json.string(p.get("networkId"));
+      if (nid.isEmpty()) return;
+      long until = System.currentTimeMillis() + COOKIE_WAIT_MS;
+      Object[] seen;
+      while ((seen = cookieSeen.remove(nid)) == null && System.currentTimeMillis() < until) { try { Thread.sleep(5); } catch (InterruptedException e) { return; } }
+      if (seen == null) return;
+      StringBuilder lines = new StringBuilder();
+      for (Object o : (List<?>) seen[0]) {
+        Map<String, Object> b = Json.object(o);
+        boolean sameSite = false;
+        for (Object r : Json.array(b.get("blockedReasons"))) if (Json.string(r).toLowerCase().contains("samesite")) sameSite = true;
+        String cl = Json.string(b.get("cookieLine"));
+        if (sameSite && !cl.isEmpty()) { if (lines.length() > 0) lines.append('\n'); lines.append(cl); }
+      }
+      if (lines.length() == 0) return;
+      for (Map<String, Object> ck : cookieParams(lines.toString(), Json.string(Json.object(p.get("request")).get("url")))) callSync("Network.setCookie", ck, s, 5000);
+    }
+
+    /** Each Set-Cookie line -> Network.setCookie's parameters, as SameSite=None and Secure; none for a host that cannot take Secure. */
+    static List<Map<String, Object>> cookieParams(String lines, String url) {
+      List<Map<String, Object>> out = new ArrayList<Map<String, Object>>();
+      java.net.URI u;
+      try { u = new java.net.URI(url); } catch (Exception e) { return out; }
+      String host = u.getHost() == null ? "" : u.getHost().toLowerCase(), scheme = u.getScheme() == null ? "" : u.getScheme().toLowerCase();
+      boolean local = host.equals("localhost") || host.endsWith(".localhost") || host.matches("127\\.\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+") || host.equals("[::1]") || host.equals("::1");
+      if (!scheme.equals("https") && !(scheme.equals("http") && local)) return out;
+      String path = u.getRawPath() == null || u.getRawPath().isEmpty() ? "/" : u.getRawPath();
+      String origin = scheme + "://" + u.getRawAuthority();
+      for (String line : lines.split("\n")) {
+        String[] parts = line.split(";");
+        int eq = parts[0].indexOf('=');
+        if (eq < 1) continue;
+        Map<String, Object> c = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+        c.put("name", parts[0].substring(0, eq).trim());
+        c.put("value", parts[0].substring(eq + 1).trim());
+        c.put("url", origin + path);
+        c.put("secure", Boolean.TRUE);
+        c.put("sameSite", "None");
+        c.put("path", path.lastIndexOf('/') > 0 ? path.substring(0, path.lastIndexOf('/')) : "/");   // (RFC 6265 5.1.4)
+        for (int i = 1; i < parts.length; i++) {
+          String a = parts[i];
+          int k = a.indexOf('=');
+          String key = (k < 0 ? a : a.substring(0, k)).trim().toLowerCase(), v = k < 0 ? "" : a.substring(k + 1).trim();
+          if (key.equals("path") && v.startsWith("/")) c.put("path", v);
+          else if (key.equals("domain") && !v.isEmpty()) c.put("domain", v);
+          else if (key.equals("httponly")) c.put("httpOnly", Boolean.TRUE);
+          else if (key.equals("max-age") && v.matches("-?\\d+")) c.put("expires", Long.valueOf(System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000 + Long.parseLong(v)));
+          else if (key.equals("expires") && !c.containsKey("expires")) {
+            try { c.put("expires", Long.valueOf(java.time.ZonedDateTime.parse(v, java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.RFC_1123_DATE_TIME).toEpochSecond())); } catch (Exception e) { /* (a session cookie then) */ }
+          }
+        }
+        out.add(c);
+      }
+      return out;
+    }

=== add src/agent/docx.mjs
new 88e04f634c6ff5964ee0e2ef
+// WORD DOCUMENTS (.docx): read, search and edit, through the Java helper in docx-helper/ (JDK only - zip + XML, no
+// library). The helper runs as ONE long-lived JVM (DocxHelper serve, the same pattern as git's service in the container):
+// a request per line on its stdin, an answer per line on its stdout, in order. It exits when we close its stdin or after
+// 15 idle minutes, and holds no document open between requests.
+//
+// The Java comes with code_boss as SOURCE (the bundle embeds it: DOCX_HELPER_SOURCES; the dev tree reads
+// docx-helper/src) and is compiled once per version into <home>/.code_boss/docx-helper/classes-<hash>, with the JDK
+// that runs here (JAVA_HOME's, else the one on PATH). A box with a JRE and no javac uses a prebuilt
+// <home>/.code_boss/docx-helper/docx-helper.jar when there is one.
+import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
+import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, rmSync, writeFileSync, renameSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { homedir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const DX_MAIN = 'com.codeboss.docx.DocxHelper';
+const DX_TIMEOUT_MS = 180000;
+
+function dxHome() { return path.join(process.env.CB_HOME || homedir(), '.code_boss', 'docx-helper'); }
+
+function dxJavaBin(name) {
+  const exe = process.platform === 'win32' ? name + '.exe' : name;
+  const jh = process.env.JAVA_HOME;
+  if (jh && existsSync(path.join(jh, 'bin', exe))) return path.join(jh, 'bin', exe);
+  return name;   // on PATH
+}
+
+/** The helper's Java sources: { "com/codeboss/docx/Doc.java": text, ... } */
+function dxSources() {
+  /* eslint-disable no-undef */
+  try { if (typeof DOCX_HELPER_SOURCES !== 'undefined' && DOCX_HELPER_SOURCES && Object.keys(DOCX_HELPER_SOURCES).length) return DOCX_HELPER_SOURCES; } catch { /* the un-bundled tree */ }
+  const dir = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'docx-helper', 'src', 'main', 'java', 'com', 'codeboss', 'docx');
+  const out = {};
+  for (const f of readdirSync(dir)) if (f.endsWith('.java')) out['com/codeboss/docx/' + f] = readFileSync(path.join(dir, f), 'utf8');
+  return out;
+}
+
+let dxClasspath = null;
+
+/** Compile the sources once per version (or find a prebuilt jar); returns the classpath. */
+function dxEnsureCompiled() {
+  if (dxClasspath && existsSync(dxClasspath)) return dxClasspath;
+  const src = dxSources();
+  const h = createHash('sha1');
+  for (const k of Object.keys(src).sort()) h.update(k + '\0' + src[k] + '\0');
+  const hash = h.digest('hex').slice(0, 12);
+  const home = dxHome();
+  const classes = path.join(home, 'classes-' + hash);
+  if (existsSync(path.join(classes, 'com', 'codeboss', 'docx', 'DocxHelper.class'))) return (dxClasspath = classes);
+  const tmp = path.join(home, `.build-${hash}-${process.pid}`);
+  rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  const files = [];
+  for (const [rel, text] of Object.entries(src)) {
+    const f = path.join(tmp, 'src', rel);
+    mkdirSync(path.dirname(f), { recursive: true });
+    writeFileSync(f, text, 'utf8');
+    files.push(f);
+  }
+  const out = path.join(tmp, 'classes');
+  mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });
+  const javac = dxJavaBin('javac');
+  const run = (flags) => spawnSync(javac, [...flags, '-encoding', 'UTF-8', '-nowarn', '-d', out, ...files], { encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true, timeout: 120000, stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
+  let r = run(['--release', '8', '-Xlint:-options']);
+  if (r.status !== 0 && /release|invalid flag/i.test(String(r.stderr) + String(r.stdout))) r = run(['-source', '1.8', '-target', '1.8']);   // a JDK 8 javac has no --release
+  if (r.error || r.status !== 0) {
+    rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });
+    const jar = path.join(home, 'docx-helper.jar');
+    if (existsSync(jar) && dxJarIsCurrent(jar)) return (dxClasspath = jar);
+    const why = r.error && r.error.code === 'ENOENT' ? `there is no Java compiler (javac) here${process.env.JAVA_HOME ? ' (JAVA_HOME is ' + process.env.JAVA_HOME + ')' : ' - set JAVA_HOME to a JDK, or put one on PATH'}`
+      : `javac failed: ${String(r.stderr || r.stdout || (r.error && r.error.message) || '').trim().slice(0, 600)}`;
+    throw new Error(`Word support could not start: ${why}.`);
+  }
+  try { renameSync(out, classes); } catch (e) { if (!existsSync(path.join(classes, 'com', 'codeboss', 'docx', 'DocxHelper.class'))) throw e; }
+  rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  // older versions' classes go
+  try { for (const d of readdirSync(home)) if (/^classes-[0-9a-f]{12}$/.test(d) && d !== 'classes-' + hash) rmSync(path.join(home, d), { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch {}
+  return (dxClasspath = classes);
+}
+
+function dxJarIsCurrent(jar) {
+  const r = spawnSync(dxJavaBin('java'), ['-cp', jar, DX_MAIN, 'version'], { encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true, timeout: 30000 });
+  try { return JSON.parse(String(r.stdout).trim()).version === '3'; } catch { return false; }
+}
+
+// ---- the service -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+let dxProc = null;          // { child, pending: Map, seq, ready: Promise }
+let dxQueue = Promise.resolve();
+
+function dxStart() {
+  const cp = dxEnsureCompiled();
+  const child = spawn(dxJavaBin('java'), ['-Xmx1g', '-cp', cp, DX_MAIN, 'serve', '15'], { stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'], windowsHide: true });
+  const st = { child, pending: new Map(), seq: 0, stderr: '' };
+  let buf = '';
+  let readyR, readyJ;
+  st.ready = new Promise((res, rej) => { readyR = res; readyJ = rej; });
+  child.stdout.setEncoding('utf8');
+  child.stdout.on('data', (d) => {
+    buf += d;
+    let i;
+    while ((i = buf.indexOf('\n')) >= 0) {
+      const line = buf.slice(0, i).replace(/\r$/, '');
+      buf = buf.slice(i + 1);
+      if (!line.trim()) continue;
+      let m;
+      try { m = JSON.parse(line); } catch { continue; }   // (a JVM warning on stdout is not an answer)
+      if (m.ready) { readyR(); if (!st.pending.size) st.hold(false); continue; }
+      const p = st.pending.get(m.id);
+      if (p) { st.pending.delete(m.id); clearTimeout(p.timer); p.resolve(m); }
+    }
+  });
+  child.stderr.setEncoding('utf8');
+  child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { st.stderr = (st.stderr + d).slice(-4000); });
+  const gone = (why) => {
+    if (dxProc === st) dxProc = null;
+    readyJ(new Error(why));
+    for (const p of st.pending.values()) { clearTimeout(p.timer); p.reject(new Error(why)); }
+    st.pending.clear();
+  };
+  child.on('error', (e) => gone(e && e.code === 'ENOENT' ? 'Word support could not start: there is no Java (java) here - set JAVA_HOME or put a JDK on PATH' : 'Word support stopped: ' + (e && e.message)));
+  child.on('exit', (code) => gone(`Word support stopped (exit ${code})${st.stderr ? ': ' + st.stderr.trim().split(/\r?\n/).slice(-3).join(' | ') : ''}`));
+  // idle, it does not keep node running; while a request waits, it does (dxHold)
+  st.hold = (on) => { try { for (const x of [child, child.stdout, child.stderr, child.stdin]) on ? x.ref?.() : x.unref?.(); } catch {} };
+  return st;
+}
+
+/** One request to the helper, answered in turn: { ok, ... } or { ok:false, code, error }. */
+export function docxCall(op, spec) {
+  const run = async () => {
+    if (!dxProc) dxProc = dxStart();
+    const st = dxProc;
+    await st.ready;
+    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+      const id = ++st.seq;
+      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+        st.pending.delete(id);
+        try { st.child.kill(); } catch {}
+        reject(new Error(`Word support did not answer in ${DX_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000} s (${op}) and was stopped; the document was not changed unless the next read shows otherwise`));
+      }, DX_TIMEOUT_MS);
+      const done = (fn) => (v) => { if (!st.pending.size) st.hold(false); fn(v); };
+      st.pending.set(id, { resolve: done(resolve), reject: done(reject), timer });
+      st.hold(true);
+      st.child.stdin.write(JSON.stringify({ id, op, ...spec }) + '\n');
+    });
+  };
+  const p = dxQueue.then(run, run);
+  dxQueue = p.catch(() => {});
+  return p;
+}
+
+export function docxStop() { try { dxProc?.child.stdin.end(); } catch {} dxProc = null; }
+
+// ---- what the agent sees -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+/** A paragraph as one line of the listing: "#12 [Heading 2] text   ‹notes›". */
+export function docxParagraphLine(p, format) {
+  const tags = [];
+  if (p.in) tags.push(p.in);
+  if (p.where) tags.push(p.where);
+  if (p.heading) tags.push('Heading ' + p.heading);
+  else if (p.style && !/^(Normal|Body Text)$/i.test(p.style) && !(p.list && /^List/i.test(p.style))) tags.push(p.style);
+  if (p.list) tags.push(p.list + (p.listLevel ? ' level ' + p.listLevel : ''));
+  const text = (format && p.formatted) ? p.formatted : p.text;
+  const shown = String(text).replace(/\t/g, '\\t').replace(/\n/g, '\\n');
+  const notes = [];
+  for (const f of p.fields || []) notes.push(`field ${String(f.code || '').split(/\s+/)[0]}: "${String(f.shows).replace(/\n/g, '\\n').slice(0, 80)}"`);
+  for (const o of p.objects || []) notes.push(`${o.object}: ${o.description}`);
+  for (const c of p.comments || []) notes.push(`comment${c.by ? ' by ' + c.by : ''}${c.on ? ' on "' + c.on + '"' : ''}: ${c.comment}`);
+  if (p.trackedChanges) notes.push('has tracked changes' + (p.deletedText ? ', deleted: "' + p.deletedText.slice(0, 80) + '"' : ''));
+  if (p.readOnly) notes.push('READ-ONLY: ' + p.readOnly);
+  return `#${p.id}${tags.length ? ' [' + tags.join(', ') + ']' : ''} ${shown}${notes.length ? '   ‹' + notes.join('; ') + '›' : ''}`;
+}
+
+export function docxListing(file, res, { format = false, heading = true } = {}) {
+  const lines = [];
+  const s = res.summary || {};
+  if (heading) {
+    const bits = [`${s.paragraphs ?? (res.paragraphs || []).length} paragraphs`];
+    if (s.tables) bits.push(`${s.tables} table${s.tables === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
+    if (s.comments) bits.push(`${s.comments} comment${s.comments === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
+    lines.push(`${file}: ${bits.join(', ')}. Edit by paragraph id (#N); find/replace use the plain text shown${format ? ' (here with formatting as **bold** *italic* <u>underline</u> ~~strike~~ `code font` [link](url) - leave those marks out of find)' : ''}; \\t is a tab, \\n a line break, ⟦…⟧ an object (keep it in a replacement).`);
+    if (s.trackedChanges) lines.push('NOTE: ' + s.trackedChanges + '.');
+    if (s.protection) lines.push('NOTE: the document is protected in Word (' + s.protection + ').');
+  }
+  for (const p of res.paragraphs || []) lines.push(docxParagraphLine(p, format));
+  return lines.join('\n');
+}
+
+/** An edit's changes as a diff-like text: - old / + new per paragraph. */
+export function docxChangesText(file, res) {
+  const out = [`--- ${file}`, `+++ ${file}`];
+  for (const c of res.changes || []) {
+    if (typeof c === 'string') { out.push('@@ ' + c); continue; }
+    out.push(`@@ ${c.id != null ? '#' + c.id : ''}${c.in ? ' (' + c.in + ')' : ''}${c.what ? ' ' + c.what : ''}`.replace(/^@@ {2}/, '@@ '));
+    const esc = (t) => String(t).replace(/\t/g, '\\t').replace(/\n/g, '\\n');
+    if (Array.isArray(c.rows)) for (const row of c.rows) out.push('+' + esc(row));
+    else if (c.before != null) { if (c.before !== '') out.push('-' + esc(c.before)); if (c.after !== '') out.push('+' + esc(c.after)); }
+    else out.push(' ' + esc(c.text));
+  }
+  return out.join('\n');
+}

+// (every response, not only a page's document: a sign-in done by a script - fetch, XHR - sets its cookie too)
+const fetchPatterns = () => ({ patterns: [{ urlPattern: '*', requestStage: 'Response' }] });
+/** How long a paused response waits for its raw headers (Network.responseReceivedExtraInfo) - cookiesBack. */
+const COOKIE_WAIT_MS = Number(process.env.CB_PANEL_COOKIE_WAIT_MS) || 50;   // (measured: they come before the pause; a moment, in case)
+/**
+ * THE COOKIES A PANEL PAGE'S RESPONSE SETS, KEPT SO THAT THEY COME BACK (prod, 2026-11-16: in the panel, WebLogic's
+ * JSESSIONID never came back and every sign-in failed with 403). A tab's page sits in an iframe on another site than
+ * code_boss's window, so to Chrome it is a third party there, and a cookie without SameSite=None is not even kept - Lax
+ * by default, Lax and Strict alike (measured: blocked as SchemefulSameSiteUnspecifiedTreatedAsLax / Lax / Strict). A
+ * Set-Cookie changed on the paused response is not taken either (measured: Chrome judged the cookies on arrival). So
+ * while the response waits, the broker sets each of its cookies itself (Network.setCookie) as SameSite=None and Secure
+ * - which Chrome allows on http://localhost and 127.0.0.1, both secure to it; another http host's are left as they are
+ * - and the page goes on only then. Each line -> the setCookie parameters, [] for none.
+ */
+export function panelCookieParams(headers, url) {
+  let u; try { u = new URL(String(url || '')); } catch { return []; }
+  const local = /^(localhost|127\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+|\[::1\])$/i.test(u.hostname) || /\.localhost$/i.test(u.hostname);
+  if (u.protocol !== 'https:' && !(u.protocol === 'http:' && local)) return [];
+  const out = [];
+  for (const h of Array.isArray(headers) ? headers : []) {
+    if (pbStr(h && h.name).toLowerCase() !== 'set-cookie') continue;
+    // (Chrome gives several Set-Cookie headers as one, their lines joined by a newline)
+    for (const line of pbStr(h.value).split('\n')) {
+      const parts = line.split(';'), first = parts[0] || '', eq = first.indexOf('=');
+      if (eq < 1) continue;
+      const c = { name: first.slice(0, eq).trim(), value: first.slice(eq + 1).trim(), url: u.origin + u.pathname, secure: true, sameSite: 'None' };
+      // (no Path: the default one, the request's folder - RFC 6265 5.1.4)
+      c.path = u.pathname.lastIndexOf('/') > 0 ? u.pathname.slice(0, u.pathname.lastIndexOf('/')) : '/';
+      for (const a of parts.slice(1)) {
+        const i = a.indexOf('='), k = (i < 0 ? a : a.slice(0, i)).trim().toLowerCase(), v = i < 0 ? '' : a.slice(i + 1).trim();
+        if (k === 'path' && v.startsWith('/')) c.path = v;
+        else if (k === 'domain' && v) c.domain = v;
+        else if (k === 'httponly') c.httpOnly = true;
+        else if (k === 'max-age' && /^-?\d+$/.test(v)) c.expires = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + Number(v);
+        else if (k === 'expires' && !('expires' in c)) { const t = Date.parse(v); if (!Number.isNaN(t)) c.expires = Math.floor(t / 1000); }
+      }
+      out.push(c);
+    }
+  }
+  return out;
+}

base bc284e6c2cad7b1b3f00c9f4
new 4584a4d0014c153399696808
@@ 370 -0 +51
+/**
+ * THE WEBLOGIC DOMAIN, AT INSTALL (the developer, 2026-11-17, after the first user outside: "building the domain isn't
+ * part of the installation, so the first time you run, it fails and you have to know to go build it before trying to
+ * start weblogic. Let's just make it part of the install."). The domain is made on the container's drive, so the
+ * container is started first; then the manager is asked to create it (to recreate one left half made) and watched
+ * until it is ready. An image without WebLogic, or a domain already there: nothing to do.
+ * { ok, detail } | { ok:false, error }.  onProgress(text) says which of the manager's steps runs.
+ */
+export async function sbcBuildDomain(st, { onProgress = () => {}, log = () => {}, waitMs = 45 * 60000, everyMs = 1500 } = {}) {
+  if (!st) return { ok: false, error: 'the container manager\'s address is not known' };
+  const look = async () => { const a = await sbcAsk(st, 'GET', '/api/container', 15000); return a && a.status === 200 && a.json ? a.json : null; };
+  let c = await look();
+  if (!c) return { ok: false, error: 'the container manager did not answer' };
+  if (!c.weblogic || !c.weblogic.inImage) return { ok: true, detail: 'This image has no WebLogic: nothing to build' };
+  if (c.weblogic.state === 'ready') return { ok: true, detail: 'The domain is already there' };
+  const until = Date.now() + waitMs;
+  if (!c.container || c.container.state !== 'running') {
+    onProgress('Starting the container (the domain is made on its drive)...');
+    if (c.container?.state !== 'starting') {
+      const s = await sbcAsk(st, 'POST', '/api/container/start', 120000);
+      if (!s || !s.json || s.json.ok === false) return { ok: false, error: `the container did not start: ${(s && s.json && s.json.error) || 'no answer'}` };
+    }
+    for (;;) {
+      await sbcSleep(everyMs);
+      c = await look();
+      const cs = c && c.container ? c.container.state : '';
+      if (cs === 'running') break;
+      if (cs && cs !== 'starting') return { ok: false, error: `the container did not start${c.container.error ? ': ' + c.container.error : ''}` };
+      if (Date.now() > until) return { ok: false, error: 'the container took too long to start' };
+      if (c && c.container && c.container.detail) onProgress(`Starting the container: ${c.container.detail}`);
+    }
+  }
+  if (c.weblogic.state !== 'creating') {
+    const how = c.weblogic.state === 'none' ? 'create' : 'recreate';   // incomplete or failed: made again from the start
+    log(`sandbox: building the WebLogic domain (${how})`);
+    const r = await sbcAsk(st, 'POST', `/api/domain/${how}`, 60000);
+    if (!r || !r.json || r.json.ok === false) return { ok: false, error: (r && r.json && r.json.error) || 'the container manager did not answer' };
+  }
+  for (;;) {
+    await sbcSleep(everyMs);
+    c = await look();
+    const w = c && c.weblogic;
+    if (!w) { if (Date.now() > until) return { ok: false, error: 'the container manager stopped answering' }; continue; }
+    if (w.state === 'ready') return { ok: true, detail: `The domain is in the container${w.port ? ` (WebLogic's console on port ${w.port})` : ''}` };
+    if (w.state === 'failed' || w.state === 'none' || w.state === 'incomplete') return { ok: false, error: w.error || `the domain was not made (it is ${w.state})` };
+    const now = (w.steps || []).find((s) => s.state === 'run');
+    if (now) onProgress(`${now.label}${now.took ? ' - ' + now.took : ''}`);
+    if (Date.now() > until) return { ok: false, error: 'building the domain took more than 45 minutes' };
+  }
+}
