+  { name: 'checks', args: '', what: 'run every check again now: the container manager, the container, the key file, whether the key works at the model endpoint (or is expired, or needs an unlock), each code_boss (answers, reaches the key store, has a key), WebLogic, free disk. Start here' },
+  { name: 'manager', args: '', what: 'what the container manager says right now, whole: the container (state, error, drive), WebLogic (domain state and its steps, the server), every code_boss instance (state, port, project, restarts and why, silence, last line), Start all' },
+  { name: 'instance', args: 'n="1"', what: 'one code_boss as it answers itself: its /api/health (windows connected, project, the key store as it reaches it, whether it has a key, its build), or that it does not answer' },
+  { name: 'log', args: 'which="diag|run|run-prev|project|bridge|weblogic|start-check|manager-state|uninstall|doctor" n="1" project="name" lines="150" grep="text"', what: 'the end of a log. diag = code_boss\'s diag.log in the container\'s home (start-up, [held] stacks, the key store\'s failures, restarts, crashes - all instances); run / run-prev = what code_boss instance n printed this run / the run before (where a crash leaves its last words); project = that project\'s .code_boss/code-boss.log; bridge = the bridge into the container (its status lines; a stalled bridge stalls everything); weblogic = WebLogic\'s server output; start-check = the container\'s start check; manager-state = the manager\'s state file; uninstall = the last uninstall; doctor = this doctor\'s own log. grep= keeps lines holding the text (case aside), two either side' },
+  { name: 'files', args: 'path="."', what: 'what is in a folder: names, sizes, when changed. A relative path is under the container folder (projects, home, .cb-run, wls, tools). The key store is not listed' },
+  { name: 'file', args: 'path="home/.code_boss/prefs.json" from="1" lines="200"', what: 'a text file, with line numbers - under the container folder, or the manager\'s folder (.code_boss in the developer\'s profile). Credentials and the key store are never readable' },
+  { name: 'env', args: '', what: 'the container\'s environment file (the manager\'s Environment tab), with secret-looking values hidden' },
+];
+
+export const DOCTOR_PROMPT = `You are the DOCTOR of a code_boss container on a developer's machine. code_boss is an AI coding agent; on this machine it runs inside a container (a Windows sandbox with its own drive letter), started and watched by the container's manager, which runs outside it. The developer opened you because something is not working - most often code_boss cannot reach the model (no key, an expired key, a locked key, the endpoint not reachable), or the key store outside the container does not answer, or a code_boss window or WebLogic does not start, or code_boss hangs. You can LOOK at everything: the manager's state, every code_boss instance, their logs and the container's files. You cannot change anything; the developer does that, and you tell them exactly what to do.
+
+HOW IT FITS TOGETHER (what to know to diagnose it)
+- The manager (Java, outside) runs the container, WebLogic's domain and server, and up to 8 code_boss instances (node, inside). Its page has Start all, the WebLogic tab, the Environment tab, and Stop / Restart per instance. It restarts a code_boss that has not answered for 3 minutes.
+- Inside, code_boss reaches the outside through ONE bridge (named pipes): its windows' traffic, WebLogic's ports, plugins' browsers, and the manager's API - which holds the KEY STORE (the API key, GitLab credentials, masking test data) outside the container, in the developer's profile. If the bridge stalls or the manager's API is busy, the store "could not be reached after 15 s" and code_boss cannot read or save the key.
+- The model endpoint is reached from inside the container with the key from the store. A 401 "Your API key has expired" means a NEW key from the portal; a 401 with an unlock_url means the key must be unlocked in a browser first.
+- code_boss writes diag.log (all instances) in the container's home; each run's printed output is kept by the manager (run / run-prev); a hold of code_boss's event loop is logged as [held] with its stack.
+
+HOW TO LOOK
+Write one or more of these tags and nothing else that needs an answer; you are then shown what each returned and asked again. Look before you answer - the checks first, then the logs that explain a failed check. Several tags in one reply are looked at together.
+
+HOW TO ANSWER
+Say what is wrong and why, from what you saw (quote the line), then what the developer should do, step by step, in the manager's or code_boss's own words (the button, the tab). If you cannot tell, say what you looked at and what would settle it. Plain prose, short. Never ask for the key or repeat one.`;
+
+const DR_SECRET_NAME = /key|token|secret|pass|cred|auth/i;
+
+/** The folder layout the doctor reads (the manager's, App.java / Server.java / Secure.java). */
+export function drPaths({ tree, secure, cbDir }) {
+  const home = path.join(tree, 'home');
+  return {
+    tree, home, cbHome: path.join(home, '.code_boss'),
+    runDir: path.join(tree, '.cb-run'),
+    projects: path.join(tree, 'projects'),
+    wlsOut: path.join(tree, 'wls', 'logs', 'out.txt'),
+    secure, keyFile: secure ? path.join(secure, 'api-key.txt') : '',
+    cbDir: cbDir || (secure ? path.dirname(secure) : ''),
+  };
+}
+
+/** KEY=VALUE lines of the container's environment file. */
+export function drEnvFile(tree) {
+  const out = {};
+  let t = '';
+  try { t = readFileSync(path.join(tree, 'home', 'env'), 'utf8'); } catch { return out; }
+  for (const raw of t.split(/\r?\n/)) {
+    const l = raw.trim();
+    if (!l || l.startsWith('#') || l.startsWith(';')) continue;
+    const eq = l.indexOf('=');
+    if (eq > 0) out[l.slice(0, eq).trim()] = l.slice(eq + 1).trim();
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+
+const drReadJson = (f) => { try { return JSON.parse(readFileSync(f, 'utf8')); } catch { return null; } };
+
+/** The end of a text file, without reading all of a big one. */
+export function drTail(file, maxBytes = 400 * 1024) {
+  let fd;
+  try {
+    const size = statSync(file).size;
+    const n = Math.min(size, maxBytes);
+    const buf = Buffer.alloc(n);
+    fd = openSync(file, 'r');
+    readSync(fd, buf, 0, n, size - n);
+    const t = buf.toString('utf8');
+    return size > n ? t.slice(t.indexOf('\n') + 1) : t;
+  } catch { return null; } finally { if (fd !== undefined) try { closeSync(fd); } catch { /* closed */ } }
+}
+
+function drLines(text, { lines = 150, grep = '' } = {}) {
+  let ls = String(text).split(/\r?\n/);
+  if (grep) {
+    const g = String(grep).toLowerCase(), keep = new Set();
+    ls.forEach((l, i) => { if (l.toLowerCase().includes(g)) for (let k = Math.max(0, i - 2); k <= Math.min(ls.length - 1, i + 2); k++) keep.add(k); });
+    ls = [...keep].sort((a, b) => a - b).map((i) => ls[i]);
+    if (!ls.length) return `(no line holds "${grep}")`;
+  }
+  const n = Math.max(1, Math.min(2000, Number(lines) || 150));
+  return (ls.length > n ? `… (${ls.length - n} earlier lines)\n` : '') + ls.slice(-n).join('\n');
+}
+
+/** Never readable: the key store and credential files, wherever they are. */
+function drForbidden(abs, P) {
+  const a = path.resolve(abs).toLowerCase();
+  if (P.secure && (a === path.resolve(P.secure).toLowerCase() || a.startsWith(path.resolve(P.secure).toLowerCase() + path.sep))) return true;
+  const b = path.basename(a);
+  return /^(api-key\.txt|gitlab-creds\.json)$/.test(b) || /\.local\.json$/.test(b) || a.split(path.sep).includes('.ssh') || a.split(path.sep).includes('secure');
+}
+
+function drResolve(p, P) {
+  const raw = String(p || '.');
+  const abs = path.isAbsolute(raw) ? path.resolve(raw) : path.resolve(P.tree, raw);
+  const inside = (root) => root && (abs.toLowerCase() === path.resolve(root).toLowerCase() || abs.toLowerCase().startsWith(path.resolve(root).toLowerCase() + path.sep));
+  if (!inside(P.tree) && !inside(P.cbDir)) throw new Error(`only the container folder (${P.tree}) and the manager's folder (${P.cbDir}) can be read`);
+  if (drForbidden(abs, P)) throw new Error('that is the key store or a credentials file: never read');
+  return abs;
+}
+
+/** GET a URL with a deadline: { status, json, text } or { error }. */
+function drGet(url, { headers = {}, ms = 5000 } = {}) {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => {
+    const u = new URL(url);
+    const r = http.request({ host: u.hostname, port: u.port, path: u.pathname + u.search, method: 'GET', timeout: ms, headers: { Host: u.host, ...headers } }, (res) => {
+      let t = ''; res.setEncoding('utf8'); res.on('data', (d) => { if (t.length < 2_000_000) t += d; });
+      res.on('end', () => { let j = null; try { j = JSON.parse(t); } catch { /* not JSON */ } resolve({ status: res.statusCode, json: j, text: t }); });
+    });
+    r.on('timeout', () => { r.destroy(); resolve({ error: `no answer in ${ms / 1000} s` }); });
+    r.on('error', (e) => resolve({ error: e.code || e.message }));
+    r.end();
+  });
+}
+
+/** The manager's address back into { base, token }. */
+export function drManagerOf(url) {
+  const m = /^(http:\/\/127\.0\.0\.1:\d+)\/\?t=([0-9a-f]+)/.exec(String(url || ''));
+  return m ? { base: m[1], token: m[2] } : null;
+}
+
+async function drManagerState(mgr) {
+  if (!mgr) return { error: 'the manager\'s address was not given' };
+  const r = await drGet(mgr.base + '/api/container', { headers: { 'X-CB-Token': mgr.token }, ms: 8000 });
+  if (r.error) return { error: r.error };
+  if (r.status !== 200 || !r.json) return { error: `HTTP ${r.status}` };
+  return { state: r.json };
+}
+
+/**
+ * The adapter the doctor's chat and its key check use: code_boss's own (gateway quirks, retries), the key read from
+ * the store's file on every call, and nothing parked - an expired or locked key is an answer here, not a wait.
+ */
+export function drAdapter({ baseURL, format, keyFile, fetchImpl }) {
+  const apiKey = () => { try { return readFileSync(keyFile, 'utf8').trim(); } catch { return ''; } };
+  const o = { baseURL, apiKey, maxRetries: 1, ...(fetchImpl ? { fetchImpl } : {}) };
+  return format === 'anthropic' ? createAnthropicAdapter(o) : createHttpAdapter(o);
+}
+
+/** What an adapter's failure means for the key: { kind: expired|unlock|rejected|unreachable|other, text, url? } */
+export function drKeyFailure(e) {
+  const status = e && (e.status || e.statusCode);
+  let body = e && e.body;
+  if (typeof body === 'string') { try { body = JSON.parse(body); } catch { /* text */ } }
+  const exp = keyExpiredMessage(body);
+  if (exp) return { kind: 'expired', text: exp };
+  const unlock = body && typeof body === 'object' ? (body.unlock_url || (body.error && body.error.unlock_url)) : null;
+  if (unlock) return { kind: 'unlock', text: 'the key needs to be unlocked in a browser first', url: unlock };
+  if (status === 401 || status === 403) return { kind: 'rejected', text: `the endpoint refused the key (HTTP ${status}): ${String((body && body.error && body.error.message) || (typeof body === 'string' ? body : '') || '').slice(0, 200)}` };
+  if (!status) return { kind: 'unreachable', text: `the endpoint could not be reached from this machine: ${String((e && (e.cause?.code || e.message)) || e).slice(0, 200)}` };
+  return { kind: 'other', text: `the endpoint answered HTTP ${status}: ${String((e && e.message) || '').slice(0, 200)}` };
+}
+
+const drAgo = (ms) => (ms == null ? '' : ms < 90000 ? `${Math.round(ms / 1000)} s ago` : ms < 5400000 ? `${Math.round(ms / 60000)} min ago` : `${Math.round(ms / 3600000)} h ago`);
+
+/**
+ * Every check, in order: [{ id, label, state: ok|warn|fail|skip, detail, fix? }] and needKey ('' | missing | expired |
+ * rejected) - the page asks for a key when it is set.
+ */
+export async function drChecks(ctx) {
+  const { P, mgr, adapter, settings } = ctx;
+  const out = [];
+  const add = (id, label, state, detail, fix = '') => out.push({ id, label, state, detail, ...(fix ? { fix } : {}) });
+  let needKey = '';
+  // 1. the manager, and the container
+  const m = await drManagerState(mgr);
+  const st = m.state || {};
+  if (m.error) add('manager', 'The container manager', 'fail', `It did not answer: ${m.error}.`, 'Open the container manager from its tray icon (or the Code Boss shortcut); if it is not running, start it with the Code Boss shortcut.');
+  else add('manager', 'The container manager', 'ok', 'It answers.');
+  const c = st.container || {};
+  if (!m.error) {
+    if (c.state === 'running') add('container', 'The container', 'ok', `Running on ${c.drive || '?'}:${c.processes ? `, ${c.processes} process(es) in it` : ''}.`);
+    else add('container', 'The container', c.state === 'starting' ? 'warn' : 'fail', `It is ${c.state || 'not known'}${c.error ? ': ' + c.error : ''}.`, c.state === 'starting' ? 'Wait for it to start.' : 'Press Start all on the manager\'s Dashboard.');
+  }
+  // 2. the key file, in the store outside the container
+  let key = '';
+  if (!P.secure) add('keyfile', 'The key file', 'skip', 'The doctor was not told where the key store is.');
+  else if (!existsSync(P.secure)) { add('keyfile', 'The key file', 'fail', `The key store folder is not there (${P.secure}).`, 'Enter your key below: the folder is made.'); needKey = 'missing'; }
+  else {
+    try { key = readFileSync(P.keyFile, 'utf8').trim(); } catch { key = ''; }
+    let when = ''; try { when = ` (saved ${drAgo(Date.now() - statSync(P.keyFile).mtimeMs)})`; } catch { /* none */ }
+    if (!key) { add('keyfile', 'The key file', 'fail', existsSync(P.keyFile) ? 'It is there but empty.' : 'There is no key in the store yet.', 'Enter your key below.'); needKey = 'missing'; }
+    else add('keyfile', 'The key file', 'ok', `There is a key in the store outside the container${when}.`);
+    // the store's size: a store over 64 MB cannot be sent to code_boss at all (Secure.java)
+    let bytes = 0;
+    const walk = (d, depth = 0) => { if (depth > 8) return; for (const e of readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) { const f = path.join(d, e.name); if (e.isDirectory()) walk(f, depth + 1); else { try { bytes += statSync(f).size; } catch { /* gone */ } } } };
+    try { walk(P.secure); } catch { /* unreadable: said by the key check */ }
+    const mb = bytes / 1048576;
+    if (mb > 64) add('storesize', 'The key store\'s size', 'fail', `${mb.toFixed(1)} MB: over the 64 MB the manager sends to code_boss - code_boss cannot read it at all.`, 'Ask the code_boss developers: the masking data in it has to be trimmed.');
+    else if (mb > 24) add('storesize', 'The key store\'s size', 'warn', `${mb.toFixed(1)} MB - every code_boss reads all of it again whenever it changes; it gets slow past this.`);
+  }
+  // 3. the key at the endpoint
+  if (key) {
+    try {
+      const models = await Promise.race([adapter.listModels(), new Promise((_, rej) => setTimeout(() => rej(Object.assign(new Error('no answer in 30 s'), { cause: { code: 'TIMEOUT' } })), 30000))]);
+      const n = Array.isArray(models) ? models.length : Array.isArray(models && models.data) ? models.data.length : null;
+      add('key', 'The key at the model endpoint', 'ok', `It works${n != null ? ` (${n} models offered)` : ''} at ${settings.baseURL}.`);
+    } catch (e) {
+      const f = drKeyFailure(e);
+      if (f.kind === 'expired') { needKey = 'expired'; add('key', 'The key at the model endpoint', 'fail', `The key has EXPIRED: "${f.text}"`, 'Create a new key in the portal and enter it below.'); }
+      else if (f.kind === 'unlock') add('key', 'The key at the model endpoint', 'warn', `The key must be unlocked first: ${f.url}`, 'Open that address in your browser, unlock the key, then run the checks again.');
+      else if (f.kind === 'rejected') { needKey = 'rejected'; add('key', 'The key at the model endpoint', 'fail', `The endpoint refused it: ${f.text}`, 'Check the key, or create a new one in the portal, and enter it below.'); }
+      else add('key', 'The key at the model endpoint', 'fail', f.text, f.kind === 'unreachable' ? 'Check the network (VPN, proxy). code_boss reaches the endpoint from inside the container the same way.' : '');
+    }
+  } else if (P.secure) add('key', 'The key at the model endpoint', 'skip', 'No key to try.');
+  // 4. each code_boss
+  const insts = (st.app && st.app.instances) || [];
+  if (!m.error && !insts.length) add('codeboss', 'code_boss', 'warn', 'No code_boss is running in the container.', 'Press Start all, or the Code Boss shortcut.');
+  for (const i of insts) {
+    const label = `code_boss ${i.id}${i.project ? ` (${path.basename(String(i.project))})` : ''}`;
+    if (i.state !== 'running') { add('codeboss-' + i.id, label, i.state === 'failed' ? 'fail' : 'warn', `It is ${i.state}${i.error ? ': ' + String(i.error).split('\n')[0] : ''}.`, i.state === 'failed' ? 'Look at its log (ask below), then Start it again on the manager\'s page.' : ''); continue; }
+    const h = await drGet(`http://127.0.0.1:${i.port}/api/health`, { headers: { Host: `localhost:${i.port}` }, ms: 5000 });
+    if (h.error || h.status !== 200 || !h.json) { add('codeboss-' + i.id, label, 'fail', `It does not answer from outside (${h.error || 'HTTP ' + h.status})${i.silentMs ? `; the manager has not heard from it for ${Math.round(i.silentMs / 1000)} s` : ''}.`, 'If this lasts, the manager restarts it after 3 minutes; or press Restart for it on the manager\'s page.'); continue; }
+    const s = h.json.store || null;
+    const bits = [`answers; ${h.json.windows || 0} window(s) connected`];
+    let state = 'ok', fix = '';
+    if (s && s.mode === 'manager' && !s.ok) { state = 'fail'; bits.push(`it cannot reach the key store${s.error ? `: ${s.error}` : ''}${s.okAgoMs != null ? ` (last reached ${drAgo(s.okAgoMs)})` : ''}`); fix = 'Ask below why: the bridge and the manager\'s logs say.'; }
+    else if (s && s.mode === 'manager') bits.push(`reaches the key store (${drAgo(s.okAgoMs)})`);
+    if (h.json.hasKey === false) { state = state === 'fail' ? state : 'warn'; bits.push('it has no key loaded'); }
+    add('codeboss-' + i.id, label, state, bits.join('; ') + '.', fix);
+  }
+  // 5. WebLogic
+  const w = st.weblogic || {};
+  if (!m.error && w.inImage) {
+    if (w.state === 'ready') add('weblogic', 'WebLogic', 'ok', `The domain is there; its server is ${(w.server && w.server.state) || 'not known'}.`);
+    else if (w.state === 'creating') add('weblogic', 'WebLogic', 'warn', 'The domain is being built.');
+    else add('weblogic', 'WebLogic', 'warn', `The domain is ${w.state}${w.error ? ': ' + w.error : ''}.`, 'Start all builds it (a few minutes), or the WebLogic tab\'s Create.');
+  }
+  // 6. disk
+  try {
+    const fs = statfsSync(P.tree);
+    const free = fs.bavail * fs.bsize, gb = free / 1073741824;
+    add('disk', 'Free disk space', gb < 0.3 ? 'fail' : gb < 2 ? 'warn' : 'ok', `${gb.toFixed(1)} GB free where the container is.`, gb < 2 ? 'Free some space on that drive.' : '');
+  } catch { /* not told */ }
+  return { checks: out, needKey, at: new Date().toISOString() };
+}
+
+export function drChecksText(r) {
+  const mark = { ok: 'OK  ', warn: 'WARN', fail: 'FAIL', skip: '--  ' };
+  return r.checks.map((c) => `${mark[c.state] || c.state} ${c.label}: ${c.detail}${c.fix ? `\n     what to do: ${c.fix}` : ''}`).join('\n') + (r.needKey ? `\n(the page asks the developer for a key: ${r.needKey})` : '');
+}
+
+/** The look tools, over the paths and the manager. */
+export function drTools(ctx) {
+  const { P, mgr } = ctx;
+  const runFile = (n, prev) => {
+    const id = Math.max(1, Number(n) || 1);
+    const names = id === 1 ? ['code-boss.txt'] : [`code-boss-${id}.txt`];
+    const f = path.join(P.runDir, names[0] + (prev ? '.prev' : ''));
+    if (existsSync(f)) return f;
+    // (older layouts: the .prev beside it named code-boss.prev.txt)
+    const alt = path.join(P.runDir, names[0].replace(/\.txt$/, prev ? '.prev.txt' : '.txt'));
+    return existsSync(alt) ? alt : f;
+  };
+  return {
+    checks: async () => drChecksText(await drChecks(ctx)),
+    manager: async () => { const m = await drManagerState(mgr); return m.error ? `The manager did not answer: ${m.error}` : JSON.stringify(m.state, null, 1); },
+    instance: async (a) => {
+      const m = await drManagerState(mgr);
+      const i = ((m.state && m.state.app && m.state.app.instances) || []).find((x) => String(x.id) === String(a.n || 1));
+      if (!i) return `There is no code_boss ${a.n || 1} (${m.error ? 'the manager did not answer: ' + m.error : 'the manager lists ' + (((m.state || {}).app || {}).instances || []).map((x) => x.id).join(', ')})`;
+      const h = await drGet(`http://127.0.0.1:${i.port}/api/health`, { headers: { Host: `localhost:${i.port}` }, ms: 5000 });
+      return `The manager says: ${JSON.stringify(i)}\nIts /api/health: ${h.error ? 'no answer - ' + h.error : `HTTP ${h.status} ${h.text.slice(0, 2000)}`}`;
+    },
+    log: async (a) => {
+      const which = String(a.which || 'diag').toLowerCase();
+      let f;
+      if (which === 'diag') f = path.join(P.cbHome, 'diag.log');
+      else if (which === 'run' || which === 'run-prev') f = runFile(a.n, which === 'run-prev');
+      else if (which === 'project') {
+        if (!a.project) { let ps = []; try { ps = readdirSync(P.projects); } catch { /* none */ } return `Which project? project="name". There are: ${ps.join(', ') || '(none)'}`; }
+        f = path.join(P.projects, path.basename(String(a.project)), '.code_boss', 'code-boss.log');
+      } else if (which === 'bridge') {
+        let bs = []; try { bs = readdirSync(P.runDir).filter((x) => /^bridge.*\.txt$/i.test(x)); } catch { /* none */ }
+        if (!bs.length) return `No bridge log in ${P.runDir} (the bridge is made the first time code_boss starts).`;
+        return bs.map((b) => `=== ${b} ===\n${drLines(drTail(path.join(P.runDir, b)) || '', a)}`).join('\n\n');
+      } else if (which === 'weblogic') f = P.wlsOut;
+      else if (which === 'start-check') f = path.join(P.runDir, 'start-check.txt');
+      else if (which === 'manager-state') f = path.join(P.cbDir, 'sandbox-manager.json');
+      else if (which === 'uninstall') f = path.join(P.cbDir, 'sandbox-uninstall.log');
+      else if (which === 'doctor') f = path.join(P.cbDir, 'doctor.log');
+      else return `There is no log "${which}". The logs: diag, run, run-prev, project, bridge, weblogic, start-check, manager-state, uninstall, doctor.`;
+      const t = drTail(f);
+      if (t == null) return `No such file: ${f}`;
+      return `${f}\n${drLines(which === 'manager-state' ? t.replace(/("token"\s*:\s*")[^"]+/gi, '$1(hidden)') : t, a)}`;
+    },
+    files: async (a) => {
+      const d = drResolve(a.path || '.', P);
+      const es = readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }).filter((e) => !drForbidden(path.join(d, e.name), P));
+      return `${d}\n` + es.slice(0, 400).map((e) => { let s = null; try { s = statSync(path.join(d, e.name)); } catch { /* gone */ } return `${e.isDirectory() ? 'dir ' : 'file'} ${e.name}${s && !e.isDirectory() ? `  ${s.size} bytes` : ''}${s ? `  ${new Date(s.mtimeMs).toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' ')}` : ''}`; }).join('\n') + (es.length > 400 ? `\n… and ${es.length - 400} more` : '');
+    },
+    file: async (a) => {
+      const f = drResolve(a.path, P);
+      const st = statSync(f);
+      if (st.isDirectory()) return `${f} is a folder: look at it with tool="files"`;
+      if (st.size > 8 * 1048576) return `${f} is ${st.size} bytes: use tool="log" for logs, or ask for a part with from= and lines=`;
+      const ls = readFileSync(f, 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/);
+      const from = Math.max(1, Number(a.from) || 1), n = Math.max(1, Math.min(2000, Number(a.lines) || 200));
+      return `${f} (${ls.length} lines)\n` + ls.slice(from - 1, from - 1 + n).map((l, i) => `${from + i}: ${l}`).join('\n');
+    },
+    env: async () => {
+      const e = drEnvFile(P.tree);
+      const ks = Object.keys(e);
+      return ks.length ? ks.map((k) => `${k}=${DR_SECRET_NAME.test(k) ? '(hidden)' : e[k]}`).join('\n') : `The container's environment file (${path.join(P.tree, 'home', 'env')}) sets nothing.`;
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+/** Save a key into the store outside the container, as the manager's Secure.put does: a temp file, then a move (retried - Windows). */
+export function drSaveKey(P, key) {
+  const k = String(key || '').trim();
+  if (!k) throw new Error('the key is empty');
+  if (/\s/.test(k)) throw new Error('a key has no spaces or line breaks in it - paste just the key');
+  mkdirSync(P.secure, { recursive: true });
+  const tmp = P.keyFile + '.doctor-' + process.pid + '.tmp';
+  writeFileSync(tmp, k + '\n', 'utf8');
+  let last = null;
+  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
+    try { renameSync(tmp, P.keyFile); return k.slice(0, 8) + '…'; } catch (e) { last = e; const t = Date.now() + 50; while (Date.now() < t) { /* a scanner holds it for a moment */ } }
+  }
+  try { rmSync(tmp, { force: true }); } catch { /* gone */ }
+  throw new Error(`could not write the key file: ${last && last.message}`);
+}
+
+/** Which endpoint, format and model: the bundle's, overridden by the container's environment file and code_boss's chosen model. */
+export function drSettings({ tree, baseURL, model }) {
+  const env = drEnvFile(tree);
+  const prefs = drReadJson(path.join(tree, 'home', '.code_boss', 'prefs.json')) || {};
+  return {
+    baseURL: String(env.AGENT_BASE_URL || baseURL || '').replace(/\/+$/, ''),
+    format: String(env.AGENT_API_FORMAT || '').toLowerCase() === 'anthropic' ? 'anthropic' : 'openai',
+    // the model chosen in code_boss's picker; else the rule code_boss uses (model-pick.mjs), from the list, once known
+    chosen: (typeof prefs.model === 'string' && prefs.model) || env.AGENT_MODEL || '',
+    model: (typeof prefs.model === 'string' && prefs.model) || env.AGENT_MODEL || model,
+  };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Serve the doctor. Resolves { url, port, close }. `html` is code_boss's page (ui.html with its config); it is served
+ * with the doctor's mark (?doctor=1 is how the page knows).
+ */
+export async function runDoctor({ html, baseURL, model, tree, managerUrl, secure, cbDir, port = 0, fetchImpl = null, idleMs = 30 * 60000, log = () => {} }) {
+  const P = drPaths({ tree, secure, cbDir });
+  const mgr = drManagerOf(managerUrl);
+  const settings = drSettings({ tree, baseURL, model });
+  const adapter = drAdapter({ ...settings, keyFile: P.keyFile, fetchImpl });
+  const ctx = { P, mgr, adapter, settings };
+  const tools = drTools(ctx);
+  const token = randomBytes(18).toString('hex');
+  const turns = [];
+  let running = null, lastSeen = Date.now();
+  const logFile = P.cbDir ? path.join(P.cbDir, 'doctor.log') : '';
+  const say = (line) => { log(line); if (logFile) { try { writeFileSync(logFile, `${new Date().toISOString()} ${line}\n`, { flag: 'a' }); } catch { /* not written */ } } };
+  const page = String(html).replace('<html', '<html data-doctor="1"');
+  let picked = !!settings.chosen;
+  const complete = async (text) => {
+    if (!picked) { picked = true; try { const p = pickDefaultModel(modelIdsOf(await adapter.listModels())); if (p) settings.model = p; } catch { /* the build's default */ } }
+    const r = await adapter.complete({ model: settings.model, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: text }], max_tokens: 4000 });
+    const c = r && r.choices && r.choices[0];
+    return (c && c.message && c.message.content) || (r && r.content && r.content.map ? r.content.map((x) => x.text || '').join('') : '') || '';
+  };
+
+  const server = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
+    const send = (code, type, body, extra = {}) => { res.writeHead(code, { 'Content-Type': type, 'Cache-Control': 'no-store', 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff', ...extra }); res.end(body); };
+    const json = (code, v) => send(code, 'application/json; charset=utf-8', JSON.stringify(v));
+    const p = server.address().port;
+    if (req.headers.host !== `127.0.0.1:${p}` && req.headers.host !== `localhost:${p}`) return send(403, 'text/plain', 'this address is not the doctor\'s own');
+    const u = new URL(req.url, `http://127.0.0.1:${p}`);
+    const cookie = /(?:^|;\s*)cbdoctor=([0-9a-f]+)/.exec(String(req.headers.cookie || ''));
+    if (u.pathname === '/' && req.method === 'GET') {
+      if (u.searchParams.get('t') !== token && !(cookie && cookie[1] === token)) return send(403, 'text/plain', 'Open the doctor from the container manager: this page needs the address the manager opens.');
+      lastSeen = Date.now();
+      return send(200, 'text/html; charset=utf-8', page, { 'Set-Cookie': `cbdoctor=${token}; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/` });
+    }
+    if (!cookie || cookie[1] !== token) return json(403, { ok: false, error: 'not the doctor\'s page' });
+    lastSeen = Date.now();
+    let body = '';
+    for await (const d of req) { body += d; if (body.length > 1048576) return json(413, { ok: false, error: 'too big' }); }
+    let b = {}; try { b = body ? JSON.parse(body) : {}; } catch { return json(400, { ok: false, error: 'not JSON' }); }
+    try {
+      if (u.pathname === '/api/doctor/checks' && req.method === 'GET') return json(200, { ok: true, ...(await drChecks(ctx)), endpoint: settings.baseURL, model: settings.model });
+      if (u.pathname === '/api/api-key' && req.method === 'GET') { let k = ''; try { k = readFileSync(P.keyFile, 'utf8').trim(); } catch { /* none */ } return json(200, { hasKey: !!k }); }
+      if (u.pathname === '/api/api-key' && req.method === 'POST') {
+        let prefix;
+        try { prefix = drSaveKey(P, b.key); } catch (e) { return json(400, { error: String(e.message || e) }); }
+        say(`a new key was saved in the store (${prefix}) - every code_boss picks it up within a few seconds`);
+        return json(200, { ok: true, prefix });
+      }
+      if (u.pathname === '/api/inspector' && req.method === 'GET') return json(200, { turns: turns.map((t) => (t.role === 'look' ? { role: 'look', label: t.label, text: t.text } : t)) });
+      if (u.pathname === '/api/inspector' && req.method === 'DELETE') { if (running) running.abort(); return json(200, { ok: true }); }
+      if (u.pathname === '/api/inspector' && req.method === 'POST' && b.reset) { if (running) running.abort(); turns.length = 0; return json(200, { ok: true }); }
+      if (u.pathname === '/api/inspector' && req.method === 'POST') {
+        if (running) return json(409, { error: 'the doctor is still answering the last question' });
+        const q = String(b.message || '').trim();
+        if (!q) return json(400, { error: 'message is required' });
+        running = new AbortController();
+        res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-ndjson; charset=utf-8', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
+        const emit = (ev) => { try { res.write(JSON.stringify(ev) + '\n'); } catch { /* the page went */ } };
+        say(`asked: ${q.slice(0, 200)}`);
+        try { await laAsk({ prompt: DOCTOR_PROMPT, toolList: DOCTOR_TOOLS, question: q, turns, tools, complete, emit, signal: running.signal, who: 'the doctor' }); }
+        catch (e) { const f = drKeyFailure(e); emit({ t: 'error', error: e && e.status ? `${f.text}${f.kind === 'expired' || f.kind === 'rejected' ? ' - enter a new key above' : ''}` : String((e && e.message) || e) }); say(`failed: ${String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 300)}`); }
+        finally { running = null; emit({ t: 'done' }); res.end(); }
+        return;
+      }
+      return json(404, { ok: false, error: `no such thing: ${req.method} ${u.pathname}` });
+    } catch (e) { return json(500, { ok: false, error: String((e && e.message) || e) }); }
+  });
+  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => { server.once('error', reject); server.listen(port, '127.0.0.1', resolve); });
+  const url = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/?t=${token}`;
+  say(`the doctor is up for ${tree} - endpoint ${settings.baseURL}, model ${settings.model}`);
+  // nobody has used it for a while, or the manager that started it is gone: it ends
+  let managerMissed = 0;
+  const timer = setInterval(async () => {
+    if (Date.now() - lastSeen > idleMs && !running) { say('not used for a while - the doctor ends'); close(); return; }
+    if (mgr) { const r = await drGet(mgr.base + '/api/status', { headers: { 'X-CB-Token': mgr.token }, ms: 5000 }); managerMissed = r.error ? managerMissed + 1 : 0; if (managerMissed >= 3) { say('the manager is gone - the doctor ends'); close(); } }
+  }, 20000);
+  if (timer.unref) timer.unref();
+  let closed = false, onClosed = () => {};
+  const done = new Promise((r) => { onClosed = r; });
+  function close() { if (closed) return; closed = true; clearInterval(timer); try { server.close(); server.closeAllConnections?.(); } catch { /* closed */ } onClosed(); }
+  return { url, port: server.address().port, close, done };
+}
+
+/** The bundle's `doctor` command: arguments in, the address printed, runs until it ends. */
+export async function runDoctorCommand(argv, { html, baseURL, model }) {
+  const arg = (n) => { const i = argv.indexOf('--' + n); return i >= 0 ? argv[i + 1] : undefined; };
+  const tree = arg('tree'), managerUrl = arg('manager'), secure = arg('secure'), cbDir = arg('cb-dir');
+  if (!tree) { console.error('doctor: --tree <the container folder> is required'); return 1; }
+  const d = await runDoctor({ html, baseURL, model, tree, managerUrl, secure, cbDir, port: Number(arg('port') || 0), log: (l) => console.error('[doctor] ' + l) });
+  console.log('DOCTOR ' + d.url);
+  await d.done;
+  return 0;
+}

=== add src/look-agent.mjs
new 9c811d99990d7c64e1f38cd7
+/**
+ * AN AGENT THAT ONLY LOOKS: a question, rounds of <look tool="x" a="1"/> tags answered by the caller's tools, then an
+ * answer. Shared by the Inspector (src/inspector.mjs: one code_boss, from its side panel) and the doctor
+ * (src/doctor.mjs: the whole container, from outside it). It holds no state and reaches nothing itself: the caller
+ * hands it its prompt, its tool list, `tools` (name -> async fn returning text) and `complete(text) -> reply text`.
+ */
+
+const LA_CAP_RESULT = 14000;   // one look's result, as sent to the model
+const LA_CAP_OLD = 400;        // what is kept of a look from an EARLIER question
+const LA_MAX_ROUNDS = 8;
+
+/** <look tool="x" a="1"/> tags in a reply -> [{ tool, args }] */
+export function laParseLooks(text) {
+  const out = [];
+  for (const m of String(text || '').matchAll(/<look\b([^>]*?)\/?>/gi)) {
+    const args = {};
+    for (const a of m[1].matchAll(/([a-zA-Z_][\w-]*)\s*=\s*"([^"]*)"/g)) args[a[1].toLowerCase()] = a[2];
+    const tool = String(args.tool || '').toLowerCase(); delete args.tool;
+    if (tool) out.push({ tool, args });
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+export const laStripLooks = (text) => String(text || '').replace(/<look\b[^>]*?\/?>/gi, '').replace(/<\/look>/gi, '').trim();
+
+const laClip = (s, n) => { const t = String(s == null ? '' : s); return t.length > n ? t.slice(0, n) + `\n… [${t.length - n} more characters not shown - narrow it, or ask for the next part]` : t; };
+
+/** The one flat message for a round: prompt, what can be looked at, this conversation, and the ask. */
+export function laAssemble({ prompt, toolList, turns, catalogLine = '', lastRound = false }) {
+  const tools = toolList.map((t) => `  <look tool="${t.name}"${t.args ? ' ' + t.args : ''}/>\n      ${t.what}`).join('\n');
+  const convo = turns.map((t) => (t.role === 'dev' ? `--- developer ---\n${t.text}` : t.role === 'you' ? `--- you ---\n${t.text}` : `--- you looked: ${t.label} ---\n${t.text}`)).join('\n\n');
+  return `${prompt}\n\nWHAT YOU CAN LOOK AT\n${tools}${catalogLine ? '\n\nSnapshot queries by number: ' + catalogLine : ''}\n\n=== THIS CONVERSATION WITH THE DEVELOPER ===\n\n${convo}\n\n=== NOW ===\n${lastRound ? 'You have used your rounds of looking. Answer now from what you have, and say what you could not establish. Write no <look> tag.' : 'Either write <look .../> tags to look at more, or answer the developer. Not both.'}`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * One question. `turns` is the conversation (mutated: the question, each look, the answer are appended). `emit(ev)` is
+ * told as it goes: { t:'look', tool, args, label }, { t:'looked', tool, label, chars, text }, { t:'answer', text }.
+ */
+export async function laAsk({ prompt, toolList, question, turns, tools, complete, prepare = async (s) => s, catalogLine = '', emit = () => {}, signal = null, who = 'the inspector' }) {
+  // what earlier questions looked at is kept as a stub: the answer that came of it is what matters now
+  for (const t of turns) if (t.role === 'look' && t.text.length > LA_CAP_OLD) t.text = t.text.slice(0, LA_CAP_OLD) + ' … [shortened: from an earlier question]';
+  turns.push({ role: 'dev', text: String(question || '') });
+  for (let round = 0; round <= LA_MAX_ROUNDS; round++) {
+    if (signal && signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped');
+    const reply = String(await complete(laAssemble({ prompt, toolList, turns, catalogLine, lastRound: round === LA_MAX_ROUNDS })) || '');
+    const looks = round === LA_MAX_ROUNDS ? [] : laParseLooks(reply).slice(0, 8);
+    if (!looks.length) {
+      const text = laStripLooks(reply) || `(${who} said nothing)`;
+      turns.push({ role: 'you', text });
+      emit({ t: 'answer', text });
+      return text;
+    }
+    const said = laStripLooks(reply);
+    if (said) turns.push({ role: 'you', text: said });
+    for (const l of looks) {
+      const label = `${l.tool}${Object.keys(l.args).length ? ' ' + Object.entries(l.args).map(([k, v]) => `${k}="${v}"`).join(' ') : ''}`;
+      emit({ t: 'look', tool: l.tool, args: l.args, label });
+      let text;
+      try { text = typeof tools[l.tool] === 'function' ? String(await tools[l.tool](l.args) ?? '') : `There is no "${l.tool}". What you can look at: ${toolList.map((t) => t.name).join(', ')}.`; }
+      catch (e) { text = `Looking failed: ${e && e.message || e}`; }
+      // decided once, here, so what is kept, what is shown and what goes to the model are the same text
+      try { text = await prepare(laClip(text, LA_CAP_RESULT), l.args); } catch (e) { text = `(withheld - preparing this result failed: ${e && e.message || e})`; }
+      turns.push({ role: 'look', label, text: text || '(nothing)' });
+      emit({ t: 'looked', tool: l.tool, label, chars: text.length, text });
+    }
+  }
+  return '';
+}
+
=== add src/model-pick.mjs
new cc1aea12467cf79b0f708ba9
+/**
+ * THE DEFAULT MODEL, from the endpoint's list (the developer, 2026-11-17: "look for the first number (which may be a
+ * decimal) and choose the highest one w/o "-withtools" in the name and w/ "argon" in the name. So current pick should
+ * be "gemini-4-argon", not "gemini-3.8-flash" or "gemini-4-hydrogen""). Used when the developer has not chosen a model
+ * themselves (a choice in the picker is remembered and wins). null when no model in the list fits.
+ */
+export function pickDefaultModel(ids) {
+  let best = null, bestN = -Infinity;
+  for (const raw of ids || []) {
+    const id = String(raw == null ? '' : (typeof raw === 'object' ? raw.id ?? raw.name ?? '' : raw));
+    const low = id.toLowerCase();
+    if (!low.includes('argon') || low.includes('-withtools')) continue;
+    const m = /\d+(?:\.\d+)?/.exec(id);
+    const n = m ? parseFloat(m[0]) : -1;
+    if (n > bestN) { best = id; bestN = n; }
+  }
+  return best;
+}
+
+/** The ids in a /models answer ({ data: [...] } or a bare list). */
+export function modelIdsOf(data) {
+  const list = Array.isArray(data && data.data) ? data.data : Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
+  return list.map((m) => (m && typeof m === 'object' ? m.id ?? m.name ?? '' : String(m))).filter(Boolean);
+}

=== change src/tokens.mjs
base 57fc4a5d27c9f5a58fdcd24a
new 81a1a66fbf8d32dc028d86b9
@@ 64 -0 +17
+/**
+ * TEXT SHORTENED WITHOUT CUTTING A TOKEN IN HALF: at most n characters, `tail` included; a token the cut would split
+ * is left out whole. (Prod, 2026-11-16: a step's label cut at 160 characters ended "#@ds.7cczlbz.SPONSOR_EDI.0~
+ * 0000000000#q5e…"; without its whole id it was no longer a token, the content filter masked its pieces, and the
+ * record showed a nested, unresolvable one.)
+ */
+export function cutTokenSafe(s, n, tail = '\u2026') {
+  const t = String(s == null ? '' : s);
+  if (t.length <= n) return t;
+  let at = Math.max(0, n - tail.length);
+  const re = new RegExp(ANY_TOKEN_SRC, 'gu'), look = t.slice(0, at + 400);
+  for (let m; (m = re.exec(look));) {
+    if (m.index >= at) break;
+    if (m.index + m[0].length > at) { at = m.index; break; }
+  }
+  return t.slice(0, at) + tail;
+}

+/**
+ * WORD JUDGES THE WORD SUPPORT (on demand, on a box with Microsoft Word: this dev box). The developer, 2026-11-17: "I never
+ * got the write to work very well. Always seemed like it was corrupting something unless it was a very small edit" - and
+ * the part that broke most: "edits that spanned across levels. So if a sentence dropped down into bold, then back up".
+ *
+ * Several hundred edits on a Word-made runbook (test/fixtures/docx/runbook.docx, or the .docx given): for every editable
+ * paragraph replaces (plain and tracked), wide replaces across every format change it has, the format-transition cases
+ * (into bold and back out, into a link, around a tab, a line break, a content control), formatting, deletes, inserts,
+ * style and paragraph changes, several edits in one call. Then WORD opens every result (repair off, alerts off), accepts
+ * all revisions and re-saves it:
+ *   - Word must open it (a file Word would "repair" is a failure),
+ *   - the text Word shows must be exactly what the edit should give,
+ *   - the formatting Word kept must be unchanged outside the edited text (and, for a format op, added on the span).
+ * Refusals (an edit that would cut a field or drop an object) are listed, not failures. About 8 s a document in Word.
+ *
+ *   node test/_docx-word-stress.mjs [file.docx] [--out dir] [--recheck] [--noword]
+ * Results stay in --out (default <tmp>/cb-docx-stress) - every edited file and Word's re-save of it.
+ */
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+const WORD_CHECK_PS1 = "param([string]$List)\n# For each file in $List: open in Word (repair off, alerts off), accept all revisions, write Word's text to\n# <file>.word.txt (paragraphs as lines), and re-save as <file>.word.docx. Prints \"OK <file>\" or \"BAD <file> :: <why>\".\n$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'\n$word = New-Object -ComObject Word.Application\n$word.Visible = $false\n$word.DisplayAlerts = 0\ntry {\n  foreach ($f in (Get-Content $List)) {\n    if (-not $f) { continue }\n    try {\n      $d = $word.Documents.Open($f, $false, $false, $false)\n      try { $d.TrackRevisions = $false } catch {}\n      try { foreach ($s in $d.StoryRanges) { $s.Revisions.AcceptAll() } } catch {}\n      try { $d.Revisions.AcceptAll() } catch {}\n      $parts = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]\n      foreach ($s in $d.StoryRanges) { $parts.Add(\"[[story \" + $s.StoryType + \"]]\"); $parts.Add(($s.Text -replace \"`r\", \"`n\")) }\n      [IO.File]::WriteAllText($f + '.word.txt', ($parts -join \"`n\"), [Text.Encoding]::UTF8)\n      $d.SaveAs2($f + '.word.docx', 16)\n      $d.Close(0)\n      Write-Output \"OK $f\"\n    } catch {\n      Write-Output (\"BAD $f :: \" + ($_.Exception.Message -replace '\\s+', ' '))\n      try { foreach ($x in @($word.Documents)) { $x.Close(0) } } catch {}\n    }\n  }\n} finally { $word.Quit() }\n";

+const read = await call('read', { docx: src, charFormats: true });

+const cases = [];
+const add = (name, spec, expect) => cases.push({ name, spec, expect });
+const uniq = (t, f) => f && t.indexOf(f) >= 0 && t.indexOf(f) === t.lastIndexOf(f);
+const words = (t) => t.replace(/⟦[^⟧]*⟧/g, ' ').split(/\s+/).filter((w) => w.length >= 4 && /^[\w.-]+$/.test(w) && uniq(t, w));
+const TRANS = ['accepts card authorisations from', 'ts card', 'card a', 'isations fr', 'card authorisations', 'card authorisations from the gateway and',
+  'them nightly. It runs on WebLogic', 'nightly', 'ly. It runs on Web', 'the gateway', 'e gate', 'gateway and', 'WebLogic 12c in', 'c in the', 'port 7001.', 't 70', '01.',
+  'tiers:\tweb', 's:\tw', 'database.\nEach', 'version: 4.2.1', 'on: 4', '4.2.1'];

+    add(`p${p.id}-replace-tracked`, { tracked: true, ops: [{ op: 'replace', id: p.id, find: w, replace: 'ZQT' + w }] }, { pid: p.id, find: w, replace: 'ZQT' + w });
+    add(`p${p.id}-bold`, { ops: [{ op: 'format', id: p.id, find: w, set: { bold: true } }] }, { pid: p.id, fmt: { find: w, add: 'b' } });
+    add(`p${p.id}-italic-tracked`, { tracked: true, ops: [{ op: 'format', id: p.id, find: w, set: { italic: true } }] }, { pid: p.id, fmt: { find: w, add: 'i' } });
+  }
+  // wide: from the 2nd char to 2 before the end, snapped out of any ⟦…⟧ - crosses whatever the paragraph has
+  let a = 2, b = t.length - 2;
+  const open = t.lastIndexOf('⟦', a); if (open >= 0 && t.indexOf('⟧', open) >= a) a = open;
+  const close = t.indexOf('⟧', b - 1); const op2 = t.lastIndexOf('⟦', b - 1); if (op2 >= 0 && (close < 0 || close >= b - 1) && t.indexOf('⟧', op2) >= b) b = t.indexOf('⟧', op2) + 1;
+  const span = t.slice(a, b);
+  if (span.length >= 4 && uniq(t, span) && !/⟦/.test(span)) {

+    add(`p${p.id}-wide-tracked`, { tracked: true, ops: [{ op: 'replace', id: p.id, find: span, replace: 'WIDE' }] }, { pid: p.id, find: span, replace: 'WIDE' });
+  }
+  for (const f of TRANS) {
+    if (!uniq(t, f)) continue;
+    const k = f.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]+/g, '_');
+    for (const [tag, rep] of [['long', 'NEW TEXT HERE'], ['empty', ''], ['same', f], ['grow', f.replace(/(\w+)$/, '$1 more')]]) {
+      add(`t${p.id}-${k}-${tag}`, { ops: [{ op: 'replace', id: p.id, find: f, replace: rep }] }, { pid: p.id, find: f, replace: rep });
+      add(`t${p.id}-${k}-${tag}-tracked`, { tracked: true, ops: [{ op: 'replace', id: p.id, find: f, replace: rep }] }, { pid: p.id, find: f, replace: rep });
+    }

+  }
+  if (!p.where && !p.in) {
+    add(`p${p.id}-delete`, { ops: [{ op: 'delete', id: p.id }] }, { pid: p.id, deleteLine: true });
+    add(`p${p.id}-delete-tracked`, { tracked: true, ops: [{ op: 'delete', id: p.id }] }, { pid: p.id, deleteLine: true });
+    add(`p${p.id}-insert`, { ops: [{ op: 'insert', after: p.id, text: 'Inserted after ' + p.id + '.' }] }, { pid: p.id, insertLine: 'Inserted after ' + p.id + '.' });
+    add(`p${p.id}-insert-tracked`, { tracked: true, ops: [{ op: 'insert', after: p.id, text: 'Tracked after ' + p.id + '.' }] }, { pid: p.id, insertLine: 'Tracked after ' + p.id + '.' });
+  }

+  }
+  writeFileSync(saved, JSON.stringify(results));

+      ok = false; why = `line ${i}: want ${JSON.stringify(A[i])} got ${JSON.stringify(B[i])}`;
+    }

+      }
+      if (bad) { fmtBad++; rows.push(`FORMAT ${r.name}: changed ${bad}`); continue; }
+    }
+  }

+process.exit(wrong || fmtBad || corrupt ? 1 : 0);

+/**
+ * THE CONTAINER'S DOCTOR (src/doctor.mjs; the developer, 2026-11-17: "something similar [to the Inspector] but that
+ * sits at the container level ... it runs checks on things that might stop the LLM from working (can it get to the api
+ * key file, is there a key, etc) and it asks for a key if there isn't one, then it's a chat w/ an agent that can see
+ * codeboss instances and logs"). Real doctor server, real page (ui.html in doctor mode) in Chrome; stand-ins for the
+ * container folder, the manager's API, one code_boss's /api/health, and the model endpoint.
+ *   1. the checks: no key -> said, and asked for; the manager, the container, a code_boss that cannot reach the store
+ *   2. the page: only with the manager's address; the checks drawn; the key asked for, saved in the store, checked again
+ *   3. an expired key: said as expired, a new one asked for
+ *   4. the chat: a question -> it looks (the checks, a log) -> an answer; the key and the store are never readable
+ *   5. the bundle: `node dist/code-boss.mjs doctor` runs outside the container and prints its address
+ * Run: node test/test-doctor.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { createServer } from 'node:http';
+import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const { runDoctor, drChecks, drTools, drPaths, drManagerOf, drAdapter, drSettings } = await import('../src/doctor.mjs');
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+// ---- the stand-ins ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+const base = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-doctor-'));
+const tree = path.join(base, 'agent'), cbDir = path.join(base, 'profile', '.code_boss'), secure = path.join(cbDir, 'secure');
+for (const d of [path.join(tree, 'home', '.code_boss'), path.join(tree, '.cb-run'), path.join(tree, 'projects', 'orders', '.code_boss'), secure]) mkdirSync(d, { recursive: true });
+writeFileSync(path.join(tree, 'home', '.code_boss', 'diag.log'), '2026-11-17T10:53:01Z  WARN  [vault] the store outside the container (masking\'s test data and exceptions) could not be reached after 15.1 s (POST /v1/secure/put): The operation was aborted due to timeout\n');
+writeFileSync(path.join(tree, '.cb-run', 'code-boss.txt'), '[code_boss] boot build=2909\n');
+writeFileSync(path.join(tree, '.cb-run', 'bridge-1.txt'), 'listening 28900\n');
+writeFileSync(path.join(tree, 'home', 'env'), 'AGENT_API_FORMAT=openai\nMY_TOKEN=supersecret\n');
+writeFileSync(path.join(tree, 'home', '.code_boss', 'gitlab-creds.json'), '{"user":"u","token":"never-readable"}');
+
+const PORTS = { mgr: 28941, inst: 28942, model: 28943 };
+const MTOKEN = 'ab'.repeat(18);
+const model = { calls: [], replies: [] };
+const EXPIRED = { error: { type: 'unauthorized', message: 'Your API key has expired. Create a new key in the portal.' } };
+const servers = [];
+function serve(port, fn) { const s = createServer(fn); servers.push(s); return new Promise((r) => s.listen(port, '127.0.0.1', r)); }
+await serve(PORTS.mgr, (req, res) => {
+  if (req.headers['x-cb-token'] !== MTOKEN) { res.writeHead(403); return res.end('{}'); }
+  res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });
+  if (req.url === '/api/status') return res.end('{"ok":true}');
+  res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, container: { state: 'running', drive: 'Z', processes: 3 }, weblogic: { inImage: true, state: 'none', error: '' }, app: { instances: [{ id: 1, state: 'running', port: PORTS.inst, project: 'Z:\\projects\\orders', silentMs: 0 }] } }));
+});
+await serve(PORTS.inst, (req, res) => {
+  res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });
+  res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, windows: 1, project: 'Z:\\projects\\orders', hasKey: false, store: { mode: 'manager', ok: false, okAgoMs: 600000, error: 'the store outside the container ... could not be reached after 15.1 s (POST /v1/secure/put)' } }));
+});
+await serve(PORTS.model, async (req, res) => {
+  let raw = ''; for await (const c of req) raw += c;
+  const auth = String(req.headers.authorization || '');
+  model.calls.push({ path: req.url, auth, body: raw });
+  if (/old-key/.test(auth)) { res.writeHead(401, { 'content-type': 'application/json' }); return res.end(JSON.stringify(EXPIRED)); }
+  res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });
+  if (req.url.endsWith('/models')) return res.end(JSON.stringify({ data: [{ id: 'gemini-3.8-flash' }, { id: 'gemini-4-argon' }, { id: 'gemini-4-hydrogen' }] }));

+const managerUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORTS.mgr}/?t=${MTOKEN}`;

+  ok(refused.every((m) => /never read|only the container folder/.test(m)), `the key, the credentials and anything outside are never read (${refused.map((m) => m.slice(0, 40)).join(' | ')})`);

+      await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => /Restart code_boss 1/.test(document.getElementById('inspLog').textContent), { timeout: 60000 });

+      ok(looked.length === 2 && /The key file/.test(looked[0].text) && /could not be reached after 15\.1 s/.test(looked[1].text) && /look\(checks\)/.test(logText) && /Restart code_boss 1/.test(logText), `a question: it looked (the checks, the log - shown as rows), and answered (${looked.map((t) => t.label).join(', ')})`);

+    } finally { await browser.close(); }
+  }

+  console.log('\n[5] the bundle runs it, outside the container');
+  const dist = path.join(ROOT, 'dist', 'code-boss.mjs');
+  if (!existsSync(dist)) console.log('  (no dist/code-boss.mjs: build first)');
+  else {
+    const env = { ...process.env }; delete env.CB_IN_CONTAINER; delete env.CB_ALLOW_OUTSIDE_CONTAINER;

+    ch.stdout.on('data', (x) => { said += x; });
+    for (let i = 0; i < 120 && !/DOCTOR http/.test(said); i++) await sleep(250);
+    const u = (/DOCTOR (http\S+)/.exec(said) || [])[1];
+    const pg = u ? await fetch(u) : null;
+    const body = pg ? await pg.text() : '';
+    ch.kill();
+    ok(u && pg.status === 200 && /data-doctor="1"/.test(body), `\`code-boss.mjs doctor\` prints its address and serves the page in doctor mode (${u || 'nothing printed'})`);
+  }
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e && e.stack || e); }
+finally { for (const s of servers) try { s.close(); } catch {} }

new e61ad00b9f39f2ea7cd138c7
+/**
+ * WORD DOCUMENTS (src/agent/docx.mjs + docx-helper/): read, search and edit a .docx through the Java helper's service.
+ * The developer, 2026-11-17: "I never got the write to work very well. Always seemed like it was corrupting something
+ * unless it was a very small edit" - and the part that broke most: "edits that spanned across levels. So if a sentence
+ * dropped down into bold, then back up". The fixture is a runbook Word made (numbered headings, a TOC, a link, a
+ * footnote, a comment, a cross-reference, a table, a content control, an image, a header and footer).
+ *   [1] read: every piece shows - tabs, line breaks, the footnote, the content control's value, fields, comments
+ *   [2] edits across formatting: only what differs changes, bold stays bold, links stay links
+ *   [3] tracked: deletions and insertions, two in one paragraph, a tracked deletion of a paragraph
+ *   [4] refusals that write nothing: an object left out, a field cut, a stale id, a TOC entry
+ *   [5] structure: insert after a heading / into a list, delete, replaceAll, style, list
+ *   [6] the package: every part not edited is the original's bytes; the service is one JVM for many calls
+ *   [8] tables: insertRow / deleteRow (with a merged cell), tracked rows, insertTable, numbered lists that restart
+ *   [9] new documents: blank with content, from a template (kept or emptied), refusals create nothing
+ *   [7] through a snippet: docxRead / docxEdit / docxCreate in the real worker, the edit's diff on the step, a token
+ *       in an edit is the real value on disk
+ * The Word-judged stress run (every edit opened by Word itself) is test/_docx-word-stress.mjs, on demand.
+ * Run: node test/test-docx.mjs
+ */
+import { copyFileSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import zlib from 'node:zlib';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-docx-home-'));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const FIX = path.join(ROOT, 'test', 'fixtures', 'docx', 'runbook.docx');
+const { docxCall, docxListing, docxStop } = await import('../src/agent/docx.mjs');
+{
+  const { spawnSync } = await import('node:child_process');
+  const jc = spawnSync(process.env.JAVA_HOME ? path.join(process.env.JAVA_HOME, 'bin', 'javac') : 'javac', ['-version'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
+  if (jc.error) { console.log('  (skipped: no JDK here - the Word helper is compiled with javac)'); process.exit(0); }
+}
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const dir = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-docx-'));
+let n = 0;
+const fresh = () => { const f = path.join(dir, `d${++n}.docx`); copyFileSync(FIX, f); return f; };
+const para = async (f, id) => (await docxCall('read', { docx: f, format: true })).paragraphs.find((p) => p.id === id);
+const shown = (p) => p.formatted || p.text;
+
+// a .docx's zip entries, by name (stored or deflated), for the byte-for-byte check
+function entries(buf) {
+  const out = new Map();
+  let e = buf.length - 22;
+  while (e >= 0 && buf.readUInt32LE(e) !== 0x06054b50) e--;   // the end of the central directory
+  let i = buf.readUInt32LE(e + 16);
+  const count = buf.readUInt16LE(e + 10);
+  for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) {
+    const method = buf.readUInt16LE(i + 10), csize = buf.readUInt32LE(i + 20), nlen = buf.readUInt16LE(i + 28), xlen = buf.readUInt16LE(i + 30), clen = buf.readUInt16LE(i + 32), local = buf.readUInt32LE(i + 42);
+    const name = buf.slice(i + 46, i + 46 + nlen).toString('utf8');
+    const start = local + 30 + buf.readUInt16LE(local + 26) + buf.readUInt16LE(local + 28);
+    const data = buf.slice(start, start + csize);
+    out.set(name, method === 8 ? zlib.inflateRawSync(data) : data);
+    i += 46 + nlen + xlen + clen;
+  }
+  return out;
+}

new 1ac9b334900b87bd21378739
+/**
+ * THE KEY ASKED FOR TWICE (the first user outside, 2026-11-17: his key had expired, he made a new one, "but then it
+ * asked for the key again"). An "expired" answer to a call that was sent with the OLD key, arriving after the new key
+ * was given, must not ask again: the adapter says which key the call went with (keyUsed), and the server sends it
+ * again with the current one when they differ.
+ *   1. the adapter passes the key each attempt was sent with, and the retry goes with the new key
+ *   2. the server's rule: keyUsed is the current key -> asked; an older key -> sent again at once, nobody asked
+ * Run: node test/test-key-renewal-stale.mjs
+ */
+import { createHttpAdapter } from '../src/llm/openai-http.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const EXPIRED = JSON.stringify({ error: { type: 'unauthorized', message: 'Your API key has expired. Create a new key in the portal.' } });
+
+console.log('\n[1] the adapter says which key the expired call went with');
+{
+  let key = 'old-key';
+  const sent = [];
+  const fetchImpl = async (url, init) => {
+    const auth = String((init.headers && (init.headers.Authorization || init.headers.authorization)) || '');
+    sent.push(auth);
+    if (/old-key/.test(auth)) return new Response(EXPIRED, { status: 401, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
+    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ data: [{ id: 'm1' }] }), { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
+  };
+  const asked = [];
+  const a = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:9/v1', apiKey: () => key, fetchImpl, maxRetries: 0,
+    onKeyExpired: (msg, info) => { asked.push(info && info.keyUsed); key = 'new-key'; return Promise.resolve(); } });
+  let r = null, err = null;
+  try { r = await a.listModels(); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+  ok(asked.length === 1 && asked[0] === 'old-key', `onKeyExpired got the key that call was sent with (${JSON.stringify(asked)})`);
+  ok(!err && sent.length === 2 && /new-key/.test(sent[1]), `and the call went again with the new key (${err ? err.message : sent.map((s) => s.slice(-7)).join(', ')})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] the server\'s rule (src/server.mjs onKeyExpired)');
+{
+  const src = (await import('node:fs')).readFileSync(new URL('../src/server.mjs', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+  ok(/onKeyExpired: \(message, \{ keyUsed \} = \{\}\) => new Promise\(\(resolve, reject\) => \{\s*\/\/[^\n]*\n[^\n]*\n\s*if \(keyUsed && session\.apiKey && keyUsed !== session\.apiKey\) \{[^}]*resolve\(\); return; \}/.test(src), 'a call that used a key already replaced is sent again, before anyone is asked');
+  ok(/a new key given in ANOTHER window[^\n]*\n\s*if \(session\.pendingKeyRenewal\) \{[^\n]*x\.resolve\(\)[^\n]*key-renewed/.test(src), 'a key given in another window releases this window\'s waiting calls and closes its question');
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+/**
+ * THE GRAY "WORKING IN THE BACKGROUND" LINE (the developer, 2026-11-16: "if the bookkeeper or heads up agent is running in
+ * the background but the main agent isn't activate the loading line, but make it gray and say 'Working in the
+ * background...', so the user knows if they're potentially waiting on a follow up response"). In the page, the model

+ *   [1] the agent's run ends; while the bookkeeper works, the line says "Working in the background", gray (not the
+ *       agent's colour); the state says sideWork
+ *   [2] the bookkeeper done: the line settles, not gray

+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+if (!puppeteer || !chrome) { console.log('  (skipped: Chrome or puppeteer-core not found)'); process.exit(0); }
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const PORT = 29900 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20);
+const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+const PROJ = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-sidework-proj-'));
+let holdMs = 7000, bkCalls = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'AGENT-REPLY done.' } }] }; },
+  async complete(req) {
+    const text = (req.messages || []).map((m) => String(m.content)).join('\n');
+    if (/You are the bookkeeper/.test(text)) { bkCalls++; await sleep(holdMs); return { choices: [{ message: { content: '<stream-ops></stream-ops>' } }] }; }
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] };
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: PROJ });
+const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+const state = async () => (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=1')).json();
+const until = async (fn, ms) => { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const v = await fn(); if (v) return v; await sleep(150); } return null; };
+const line = (page) => page.evaluate(() => { const r = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .status')].pop(); return r ? { text: r.textContent, gray: r.classList.contains('status-gray'), color: getComputedStyle(r).color } : null; });
+try {
+  const page = await browser.newPage();

+  await page.waitForFunction(() => document.getElementById('input'), { timeout: 20000 });

+  const during = await until(async () => { const s = await state(); const l = await line(page); return s.status !== 'running' && l && /Working in the background/.test(l.text) ? { s, l } : null; }, 15000);
+  ok(!!during && during.l.gray, `the run over, the bookkeeper working: "${during ? during.l.text.trim().slice(0, 50) : 'no such line'}", gray`);
+  ok(!!during && during.s.sideWork === true && bkCalls >= 1, 'the state says so too (sideWork)');
+  const accent = await page.evaluate(() => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--accent-text').trim());
+  ok(!!during && during.l.color !== accent, `not the agent's colour (${during && during.l.color})`);

+

+finally { await browser.close(); server.close(); }

+/**
+ * A SKILL'S HELPER, EDITED, IS LOADED AGAIN (prod, 2026-11-16: an edited _helper.mjs that a skill imports was never
+ * loaded again - Node kept the first copy until a restart), and a skill has api.sleep.

+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-skh-home-'));

+const dir = path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'skills'); mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
+const touch = (f, t) => utimesSync(f, t, t);
+let t = Date.now() / 1000 - 100;
+const put = (name, text) => { const f = path.join(dir, name); writeFileSync(f, text); touch(f, t += 5); };
+put('_inner.mjs', 'export const deep = "deep-1";\n');
+put('_h.mjs', 'import { deep } from "./_inner.mjs";\nexport const word = () => "one/" + deep;\n');
+put('greet.mjs', 'import { word } from "./_h.mjs";\nexport const description = "says a word";\nexport default async function () { return word(); }\n');
+

+const g = list.find((s) => s.name === 'greet');
+ok(!!g && (await runSkill(g, {}, {})) === 'one/deep-1', 'the skill runs with its helper');
+put('_h.mjs', 'import { deep } from "./_inner.mjs";\nexport const word = () => "two/" + deep;\n');
+ok((await runSkill(g, {}, {})) === 'two/deep-1', 'the helper edited: the same skill, run again, has the new helper');
+
+console.log('\n[2] loaded afresh; a helper of a helper');
+put('_inner.mjs', 'export const deep = "deep-2";\n');
+const again = (await loadSkills({ projectDir: proj })).list.find((s) => s.name === 'greet');
+ok((await runSkill(again, {}, {})) === 'two/deep-2', 'a helper the helper imports, edited: loaded again too');
+ok((await runSkill(g, {}, {})) === 'two/deep-2', 'and the entry from before sees it as well');
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
